# exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Run: 20261011T210330Z-fleet05-A001-quantifier-audit. Agent A001, stream B01. Artifact: source-bound quantifier audit. Full FEI remains OPEN. Independent acceptance of this audit is pending.

The target is

\[
\exists C\in[0,\infty)\ \forall n\in\mathbb N_0\ \forall f:\{-1,1\}^n\to\{-1,1\},\qquad H_2(f)\le C I(f).
\]

Here \(\widehat f(S)=2^{-n}\sum_x f(x)\prod_{i\in S}x_i\), \(P_f(S)=\widehat f(S)^2\), \(H_2(f)=\sum_SP_f(S)\log_2(1/P_f(S))\), and \(I(f)=\sum_S|S|P_f(S)=\sum_i\Pr[f(X)\ne f(X^{\oplus i})]\). Set \(0\log(1/0)=0\). Constants, including the zero-dimensional cube, have H=I=0. Ratios are taken only for nonconstant functions, equivalently I>0. The assertion \(\forall n\exists C_n\) is weaker and elementary; it is not FEI.

Equivalently, \(C_{\rm FEI}=\sup_{n,f:I(f)>0}H_2(f)/I(f)<\infty\). A disproof requires actual Boolean signs \(f_j\) with \(H_2(f_j)/I(f_j)\to\infty\). Refuting a proposed constant, a local payment, or a non-Boolean relaxation does not satisfy this target. Natural-log entropy is \(H_{\rm nat}=(\ln2)H_2\).

Use \(D_{\rm char}=\sum_i\min(I_i,1-I_i)\), \(v=\operatorname{Var}(f)\), and \(\delta=I-v\). These are distinct from dual total correlation, physical pivotal influence under biased measures, and the influence drop of a restriction. Uniform H+ is the nonempty-atom contribution; under biases it includes the product-basis correction and can be negative.

The percolation paper is an additional requested source, not a substitute for R. Its lower bounds, singularity, transport and thinning statements have no implicit edge to universal FEI. T's read-k DNF concentration corollary is downstream of T06 and introduces no additional sufficient condition for full FEI.

R's monotone dual-rail embedding (biased.tex biasfull:dualrail) is another failed transfer shortcut, not a universal reduction with paid cost. Conditioning independent rail pairs to decode fair bits has valid-event probability at most 2^(-n) and KL cost at least n ln2. That explicit dimension cost must be paid before importing a monotone class theorem. The statement concerns this embedding, not all monotone encodings. The weighted-threshold normalization no-gos in biasfull:tribes, biasfull:mixture, the failure theorem and local-TC proposition similarly refute precise proposed physical-TC/pivotal-credit payments, rather than the weighted corrected FEI+ target T15 itself.

The supplement introduces further targets beyond these manuscripts: mismatch/KL tree payment (G-R-MISMATCH), actual-source history transfer, weighted reveal lifetime, full signed Booleanity and fixed-cyclic conditional completion. Their quantifier audit follows below; none is accepted as a new universal premise.

**Endpoint J explicitly.** With one fixed representation and global lambda, set pi_l=min(Pr(h_l=1),1/2), Lambda=sum log2(1+lambda_l pi_l), Z=1-2^(-Lambda), and Kref=3+2log2 3+log2(6e). For actual partition runs with subsets A_l partitioning A,

\[
B_\lambda=\sum_{l\in\mathcal F}[\log_2(1/\lambda_l)+s_l\log_2 M_l+(6/5)s_l\log_2(\kappa_l/s_l)]
 +\sum_{l\in\mathcal R}\log_2(1/\pi_l)+(6/(5\ln2))\sum_{j\in A}F_{\tau(j)}^j(A).
\]

The source proves \(H\le h_2(v)+K_{\rm ref}I+R_f\), with

\[
R_f=v(\Lambda+\log_2 Z)+\sum_{A\ne\varnothing}P_f(A)
 [\mathbb E_{\mathcal P_A}B_\lambda(A;r)-H_2(\mathcal P_A)].
\]

Thus the unpaid line is \(\exists K<\infty\ \forall f\ \exists\text{admissible global representation, weights, conditional actual-run laws}:R_f\le KI(f)\). The packing constraint is sum_(l:j in R_l) lambda_l<=1 for every j. Laws may depend on A, but the reference generator and weights remain fixed for f. Actual runs must be distinct; counting abstract descriptions twice fabricates posterior credit. Conditioning contributes v log Z, which is nonpositive and cannot be dropped in a claim of exact equality. Since h2(v)<=2I, the forward constant is 2+Kref+K. No equivalence of optimal constants for this witness family is asserted.

**Good-coordinate equivalence reconstructed.** The exact Fourier split gives Delta_i H=B_i+M_i, 0<=M_i<=(2/ln2)I_i, and I=I_i+average child I. If every nonconstant reached function admits relevant i with B_i<=K I_i, induction starting at constant leaves proves H<=(K+2/ln2)I. Conversely conditioning reduces entropy in each term of a chain rule, so sum_i B_i<=H. If H<=C sum_i I_i and every positive-I coordinate had B_i>C I_i, summing would contradict this inequality; zero-I coordinates have B_i=0. This proves existence, with K=C, for each function. The reverse implication uses FEI as an explicit assumption; it cannot justify the selection premise in a purported FEI proof. There is no proved equality of the two optimal constants.

**Scalar and centered routes.** Concavity bounds Delta_i H<=kappa(I_i), but kappa(u)/u=log2(1/u)+3/ln2+O(u) diverges near zero. A restriction-closed class with a uniformly available I_i>=tau can use kappa(tau)/tau; availability is a structural assumption. Centering gives a nonnegative extra budget and a telescope, but low-influence nodes have none of that gain. The bent splice refutes all-coordinate bounded pricing and the source's diluted splice refutes maximum-I and maximum-dD selection; neither refutes existential R06.

**Tree and supplement mismatch.** For a finite uniform physical restriction tree with visitation probability pi_v, H(root)=sum pi_v Delta_v H+sum pi_leaf H(leaf), and the same formula holds for I with queried coordinate influences. Proving sum pi_v B_v<=K sum pi_v I_query plus leaf H<=L I_leaf suffices with coefficient max(K+2/ln2,L). A stronger source bound uses mismatch mass mu=sum(|ahat|-|bhat|)^2/4 and the charge mu[log2(1/mu)-D2(nu||q)+3/ln2]. The linear part is already paid by influence; the entire remaining logarithmic tree sum **and terminal entropy** still need payment. This mu is different from B05's influence-variation refund M. The B05 reviewed family refutes fixed-price L<=Kd+AV+BM at every split, while child budgets can remain ample. Its no-go is consumed as a route correction, not an FEI disproof.

**Elementary optimum-preserving reductions reconstructed.** On disjoint blocks, tensor spectra are products, so H(f^tensor t)=tH(f), I(f^tensor t)=tI(f). Balancing by a fresh sign gives g_t=z f^tensor t, H(g_t)=tH(f), I(g_t)=tI(f)+1. Hence balanced functions have the same optimal FEI constant as all functions, as an extended-real supremum. Character multiplication permutes atoms by symmetric difference and replaces I_i by either I_i or 1-I_i; consequently universal H<=C I is equivalent, at that same C, to universal H<=C Dchar. Lemma 2.2 then gives H<=4C(I-v). Conversely universal H+<=K(I-v) implies FEI(max(K,log2 e)); these strong/nonempty formulations are equivalent in finiteness, without an assertion of equal optimum.

For balanced g, M_g=((1+g)/2)y1+((1-g)/2)y2 has H=2+H(g)/2 and I=1+I(g)/2. Applying a selector bound C to g_t gives

\[
tH(f)+4\le C(tI(f)+3).
\]

Divide by t and let t tend to infinity. This gives H(f)<=C I(f); the reverse direction is subset inclusion. No additive loss remains. For rarity, F=1+A_r(g-1), p=2^(-r),

\[
H(F)=pH(g)+2rp(2-p)+(1-p)^2\log_2\frac1{(1-p)^2},\qquad I(F)=p[I(g)+r].
\]

Take g=g_t and r=ceil(2log2(t+1)). Then I(F)->0, Pr(F=-1)=p/2->0, r/t->0, and the final entropy term divided by p tends to 2log2 e. Therefore H(F)/I(F)->H(f)/I(f) for every nonconstant seed. Inclusion and approximation establish equality of suprema, also if the supremum is infinite. These are completed implications, not proofs that the common supremum is finite. R01-R02 add coding and smoothing; their source derivations are recorded as recovered baseline, with independent reconstruction requested from A002/A094 rather than silently claimed here.

**Dyadic gate-potential equivalence reconstructed at its actual domain.** Assume FEI(C); define Gamma_C(p)=inf over finite uniform Boolean g of probability p of [C I(g)-H(g)]. For dyadic p the admissible set is nonempty, Gamma is finite/nonnegative, symmetric, endpoints zero, and a dictator gives Gamma(1/2)<=C. With finitely many disjoint child encoders approaching their infima, raw composition yields

\[
C I(F)-H(F)=\sum_i\frac{I_i^\nu(G)}{V_{p_i}}
 [C I(g_i)-H(g_i)+h_2(V_{p_i})]-H_\nu(G).
\]

The left side is at least Gamma(pout). Taking the finite collection of approximation limits proves R09. Conversely apply R09 to any Boolean G with all inputs fair: V=1, h2(V)=0, and Gamma(pout)>=0 immediately imply H(G)<=C I(G). Nondyadic output probabilities have no finite uniform encoder; neither continuity nor an all-real potential follows merely from this infimum. A proposal defining Gamma through C I-H while assuming it nonnegative has not paid the conjecture.

**Fixed conditional marginal admission.** Fix any actual Boolean base F0(X,Y0) realizing a specified marginal on spectral X, with X degree <=d if needed. For an arbitrary Boolean seed f on fresh Z blocks put F_t=F0 f^tensor t. Its X marginal is unchanged, its X degree is unchanged, and

\[
H(\mathcal S_{Y0,Z}\mid\mathcal S_X)
=H_{F0}(\mathcal S_{Y0}\mid\mathcal S_X)+tH(f),\quad
I(F_t)=I(F0)+tI(f).
\]

Therefore a universal bound on this conditional entropy by C I(F_t), even restricted to that one fixed realized marginal and admitting arbitrary data tensors, implies full FEI(C) after division by t. Full FEI(C) implies the conditional bound since conditional entropy<=full entropy. The tensor-stable endpoint thus has the same optimum, once a finite actual Boolean base exists. This proof establishes the admission test without depending on a missing cyclic-source proof. The campaign's particular cyclic realization is navigation-only here: no unbundled source is certified as read. Bounds using only I_Y or an additive d term still need their own exact constant ledger. The marginal bound H(p_X)<=(2/ln2)I_Y+2(d-1) does not pay the conditional term.

**History/reveal distinction.** The supplemental map records H_nat-3I<=C_path<=H_nat+I. A universal C_path<=K I would imply FEI with bit coefficient (K+3)/ln2; FEI(C) would bound C_path by (C ln2+1)I. This is equivalent in finiteness if those source identities are available, not a new independently audited theorem in this run. Separately the weighted reveal target integral exp(t) E TC(P_t) dt<=A_eps[DTC(P0)+J0] retains the negative forced-information refund and concerns natural units under its precise policy. Even a proof of that lifetime bound leaves actual-source entropy/history transfer unpaid. Finite integral in each fixed dimension is not a uniform bound.

**Geometric payment.** Refinement is a genuine upper-bound mechanism: with fixed b,M, H(A) is at most root entropy plus log2(2^b-1) times total internal expected occupancy and log2(2^M-1) times leaf expected occupancy. For crossing sets, arm bounds give E N_s<=C m(R)/m(s); lower power growth makes the reciprocal-scale sum finite uniformly, and I>=c m(R) converts to FEI. Its first universal gap is a dimension-independent occupancy hierarchy and root/terminal payment for arbitrary Boolean spectra, not the chain rule. Pivotal entropy lower bounds and equal one/two-coordinate marginals do not supply this missing upper bound.

# exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

A001/B01 stage 2, run 20261011T231625Z-fleet05-A001-quantifier-audit. All physical inputs are independent uniform bits, signs are Boolean, logarithms are base two. This stage computes the exact minimum over relevant physical coordinates of B_i/I_i and Delta_i H/I_i for the lead's X009 and B05 families. B_i=H(S_i conditional on S_without_i); Delta_i H=H(f)-[H(f_i^+)+H(f_i^-)]/2. These quantities differ by the nonnegative actual-child Jensen-Shannon term. No function with I_i=0 is divided by I_i.

**Completed mathematical result, pending independent review:** For every even m>=4, both X009 minima occur at every data coordinate. Their limits as m grows are 2 and 4 respectively. For canonical B05 with even k>=4 and r=2k controls, the erasure minimum occurs at data coordinates for k=4 and controls for k>=6 (A089's prior result, independently reconstructed here at r=2k). The entropy-decrement minimum occurs at data coordinates for k=4,6,8,10,12 and controls for every even k>=14. The selector is never minimizing. All minima are given below by closed expressions, not floating comparisons. In particular a bounded-ratio coordinate exists for both families in every dimension: X009 has B/I<2, Delta/I<4; B05 has a selectable decrement bound below 4+41/512. Full FEI is OPEN.

The universal target remains exists one finite C, for every finite dimension n and every Boolean sign f, H_2(f)<=C I(f); constants have H=I=0. This stage does not establish a uniform existential rule for all Boolean f or a hereditary stopping policy.

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

## 1. Signed-pair functionals

For nonnegative u,v define

\[
\Phi(u,v)=(u+v)h_2(v/(u+v)),\qquad \Phi(0,0)=0.
\]

For real coefficients a,b define

\[
\mathcal D(a,b)=\eta(a^2)+\eta(b^2)
-\tfrac12\eta((a+b)^2)-\tfrac12\eta((a-b)^2),\qquad
\eta(q)=-q\log_2 q,\quad\eta(0)=0.
\]

For every coordinate i, group the Fourier coefficients into pairs (a,b)=(fhat(T),fhat(T+i)), T not containing i. Then

\[
B_i=\sum_T\Phi(a^2,b^2),\quad
\Delta_iH=\sum_T\mathcal D(a,b),\quad I_i=\sum_T b^2.
\]

Indeed restriction produces coefficients a+b and a-b. The first identity is conditional entropy in the parent law, the second is the exact entropy difference. Both functionals are symmetric in their arguments; D is invariant under either sign change because it interchanges the two restricted squares. Both are homogeneous of degree two in coefficient amplitudes. Directed multiplicities still matter for I_i: the coefficient with i present must remain the second component.

Write c=log2(e)=1/ln2; elementary atanh-series bounds give 7/5<c<3/2. The tools used below are Phi(u,v)<=u+v, Phi(u,v)>=2min(u,v), and, for u>=v>0,

\[
\Phi(u,v)\ge v[\log_2(u/v)+1].\tag{1}
\]

The last inequality follows from u log2(1+v/u)>=v. If u+v<=1, Phi(u,v)<=v log2(e/v). For each pair,

\[
0\le\mathcal D(a,b)-\Phi(a^2,b^2)\le2c\min(a^2,b^2).\tag{2}
\]

The lower counterpart is

\[
\mathcal D(a,b)-\Phi(a^2,b^2)
\ge 2c\frac{a^2b^2}{a^2+b^2},\tag{3}
\]

from 1-h2((1+z)/2)>=c z^2/2 at z=2ab/(a^2+b^2). These inequalities follow by integrating atanh z>=z and atanh z<=z/sqrt(1-z^2); (2) is the exact C005 bound applied to one pair.

## 2. One common core and its multiplicities

Let k>=4 be even, N=2^k, delta=1/N, s=1/sqrt(N), and

\[
b(x)=(-1)^{x_1x_2+\cdots+x_{k-1}x_k},\quad
h(x,y)=b(x)-2\mathbf1_{x=0}\mathbf1_{y=1}.
\]

This h is X009 (put k=m). Its bent coefficients c_T are +/-s with c_empty=c_singleton=s. Let mu=s-delta, a=1-mu. The h coefficients are

\[
\widehat h(T,\varnothing)=c_T-\delta,\qquad
\widehat h(T,\{y\})=\delta.
\]

Put N_+=(N+sqrt(N))/2 and N_-=(N-sqrt(N))/2. For any data coordinate, the directed no-selector coefficient pairs have counts

\[
\begin{array}{c|c}
(\mu,\mu)&N/8+\sqrt N/4\\
(s+\delta,s+\delta)&N/8-\sqrt N/4\\
(\mu,s+\delta)&N/8+\sqrt N/4\\
(s+\delta,\mu)&N/8-\sqrt N/4
\end{array}
\]

where absolute amplitudes suffice for entropy. The selector-containing part adds N/2 pairs (delta,delta). For explanation, toggling data bit j in the Fourier index changes the quadratic phase precisely when its partner bit is one. Fixing that partner to zero or one leaves a bent form on k-2 other coordinates with sign imbalance sqrt(N)/2; hence the directed counts above. In particular the mixed directed counts are not equal even though the two entropy functionals are symmetric. The initial implementation got this direction wrong, was rejected, and is documented in out/FIRST_CHECK_FAILURE.md.

Define the two data costs

\[
\beta=\tfrac12-\tfrac\delta2
+\frac{\Phi((s-\delta)^2,(s+\delta)^2)}{4\delta},\qquad
\gamma=1-\delta+\frac{\mathcal D(s-\delta,s+\delta)}{4\delta}.\tag{4}
\]

Every data coordinate of h has B_x=beta, Delta_x H=gamma, I_x=1/2+delta. Its selector has I_y=delta and

\[
B_y=N_+\Phi(\mu^2,\delta^2)+N_-\Phi((s+\delta)^2,\delta^2),\tag{5}
\]

\[
\Delta_yH=N_+\mathcal D(\mu,\delta)+N_-\mathcal D(s+\delta,\delta).\tag{6}
\]

All expressions are exact finite logarithmic expressions in the integer parameter; no large-cube enumeration is needed to define them.

## 3. X009: exact all-parameter minima

Because a bit has conditional entropy at most one, beta<=1. Thus beta/(1/2+delta)<2. The exact scalar split bound Delta_i H<=2 gives gamma/(1/2+delta)<4.

For every selector pair, (a^2+b^2)/b^2 >=N-2sqrt(N)+2>=N/2, since (sqrt(N)-2)^2/2>=0. Keeping only the b^2 log2((a^2+b^2)/b^2) term in Phi and summing yields B_y/I_y>=k-1. This excludes the selector from the erasure minimum for every k>=4. It also excludes it from the decrement minimum for k>=6, since Delta_y>=B_y. At k=4 the exact rational log certificate gives Delta_y/I_y>8 while gamma/I_x<4. Consequently

\[
\min_i\frac{B_i}{I_i}=\frac\beta{1/2+\delta},\qquad
\min_i\frac{\Delta_iH}{I_i}=\frac\gamma{1/2+\delta},\tag{7}
\]

and all data coordinates, only, attain both minima. As s->0, Phi((1-s)^2,(1+s)^2)->2 and D(1-s,1+s)->4; (4) gives beta->1, gamma->2. Thus the limits of (7) are 2 and 4. These are sharp suprema on X009, without being a lower bound of 4 on the optimal FEI constant.

## 4. B05: exact costs for every coordinate type

Put r=2k, p=2^(-r)=delta^2, t=pa, and let A(z)=1_(z=0) on r fresh controls. The original B05 sign is

\[
F(x,y,z)=1+A(z)(h(x,y)-1).
\]

Its coefficient at the completely empty index is 1-t. At nonempty control indices and empty core index it is -pa; at nonempty core index it is p hhat, for every control fiber. Hence activation changes the empty-core pair in every control fiber, not just the completely empty Fourier index.

The exact data costs and influence are

\[
B_x=p(\beta-2\mu^2)+(p-p^2)\Phi(a^2,\mu^2)
+\Phi((1-t)^2,p^2\mu^2),\quad I_x=p(1/2+\delta),\tag{8}
\]

\[
\Delta_xH=p(\gamma-4\mu^2)+(p-p^2)\mathcal D(a,\mu)
+\mathcal D(1-t,p\mu).\tag{9}
\]

For any control,

\[
B_z=2t-2t^2+\Phi((1-t)^2,t^2),\quad
\Delta_zH=4t-4t^2+\mathcal D(1-t,t),\quad I_z=t.\tag{10}
\]

Finally its selector has I_y=p delta and

\[
B_y=p[(N_+-1)\Phi(\mu^2,\delta^2)+N_-\Phi((s+\delta)^2,\delta^2)]
+(p-p^2)\Phi(a^2,\delta^2)+\Phi((1-t)^2,p^2\delta^2),\tag{11}
\]

\[
\Delta_yH=p[(N_+-1)\mathcal D(\mu,\delta)+N_-\mathcal D(s+\delta,\delta)]
+(p-p^2)\mathcal D(a,\delta)+\mathcal D(1-t,p\delta).\tag{12}
\]

Equations (8)-(12) come from the coefficient-pair multiplicities, not by assuming that Fourier spectra mix incoherently. The terms (p-p^2) retain all nonempty control fibers. Formula (9) is the new decrement counterpart of A089's erasure formula; (10)-(12) similarly retain the actual-child entropy costs.

## 5. B05 erasure: consumed prior theorem, independent subclaim check

A089 already proves that the erasure minimum is data at k=4 and control for even k>=6, for the more general r>=2k domain. Here is an independent reconstruction at the required r=2k.

The core obeys 1-c delta<=beta<=1-c delta/(1+delta), obtained from the two scalar entropy deficit bounds for z=2s/(1+delta). Since a/mu>1/s, (1) gives Phi(a^2,mu^2)>=(k+1)mu^2. Dropping the nonnegative last term in (8),

\[
\frac{B_x}{I_x}\ge2+\frac\delta{1/2+\delta}
 [((k+1)(1-p)-2)(1-s)^2-c-2].\tag{13}
\]

For k>=6 the bracket is greater than 8/25, by substituting k=6,s=1/8,p=1/4096,c<3/2; both positive factors increase afterward. Since 1/2+delta<=33/64, (13) is greater than 2+(3/5)delta.

For controls, (10) and Phi(u,v)<=v log2(e/v), t>=p/2, t<=p imply

\[
\frac{B_z}{I_z}\le2+\delta^2(4+4k)
\le2+(7/16)\delta\qquad(k\ge6).\tag{14}
\]

Also B_z/I_z>=2. The selector's nonempty data fibers have the same q/e>=N/2 lower bound as X009, but carry only 1-delta of the selector influence. Thus B_y/I_y>=(1-delta)(k-1)>=45/16, greater than the uniform control upper bound 2+20/256. At k=4 the interval certificate gives B_x/I_x<2<B_z/I_z and B_y/I_y>5. Therefore the erasure argmin is exactly as claimed by A089. This is acceptance of its B05 r=2k subclaim, not a second discovery and not acceptance of its A062 result.

## 6. B05 decrement: different argmin and an all-parameter tail proof

Let chi(t)=4-4t+D(1-t,t)/t. For t<=p<=1/256, (2) and Phi((1-t)^2,t^2)<=t^2 log2(e/t^2) show

\[
\chi(t)\le4+t[2\log_2(1/t)+3c]
\le4+\delta^2(4k+9/2)\le4+41/512.\tag{15}
\]

The function t[2log2(1/t)+3c] increases on this interval (its derivative is 2log2(1/t)+c>0), and delta^2(4k+9/2) decreases through even k>=4. Moreover chi(t)>4: (1) gives Phi((1-t)^2,t^2)>=t^2[2log2((1-t)/t)+1]>15t^2>4t^2, using (1-t)/t>=255 and log2(255)>7. Therefore every control ratio exceeds 4 but stays uniformly bounded by (15).

The selector lower bound in Section 5 excludes it from the decrement minimum for k>=6, because (1-1/64)*5>4+41/512. At k=4 its certified decrement ratio exceeds 8, while the data ratio is below 4.

For k=4,6,8,10,12 the strict data-versus-control decrement ordering is certified by out/ORDER_CERTIFICATES.json. The data ratio is <4 in these five cases, the control ratio >4. This establishes a genuine finite base, not an assumed pattern.

For all even k>=14 we prove the opposite order. First an exact lower bound for the core is

\[
\gamma\ge2-\delta(k/2+3c/2)>2-\delta(k/2+9/4).\tag{16}
\]

Indeed define G(s)=2[(1+s)^2 ln(1+s)+(1-s)^2 ln(1-s)]. Then G(0)=G'(0)=0 and G''(s)=12+4ln(1-s^2)<=12, so G(s)<=6s^2. Insert this into D(1-s,1+s) and (4) to get (16), retaining the explicit log delta term.

For k>=14, s<=1/128, a>=1-s, mu<=s, so a^2/(a^2+mu^2)>27/28. Combining (1) and (3), using c>7/5 and a/mu>1/s,

\[
\mathcal D(a,\mu)\ge\mu^2[k+1+2c a^2/(a^2+\mu^2)]
>\mu^2(k+37/10).\tag{17}
\]

Drop the nonnegative last term in (9), combine (16)-(17), and subtract 4(1/2+delta). This yields

\[
\frac{\Delta_xH}{p}-4(1/2+\delta)
>\delta\{[(k+37/10)(1-p)-4](1-s)^2-k/2-25/4\}.
\tag{18}
\]

For k>=14 the braced expression is bounded below by

\[
[(k+37/10)(1-2^{-28})-4](127/128)^2-k/2-25/4.
\]

This is linear in k with positive slope. At k=14 it exceeds 1/5 by an exact Fraction comparison saved in out/TAIL_ANCHORS.json. Consequently the data decrement ratio is greater than 4+delta/5 (in fact the stronger excess is delta/[5(1/2+delta)]). On the other hand (15) gives the control ratio less than 4+delta/100 for every k>=14: delta(4k+9/2) decreases and at k=14 equals (121/2)/16384<1/100. Hence controls strictly minimize for every even k>=14, with no finite-to-infinite extrapolation.

The exact minima for B05 are therefore

\[
\min_i B_i/I_i=\begin{cases}B_x/[p(1/2+\delta)]&k=4,\\B_z/t&k\ge6,\end{cases}
\quad
\min_i\Delta_iH/I_i=\begin{cases}\Delta_xH/[p(1/2+\delta)]&4\le k\le12,\\\chi(t)&k\ge14,\end{cases}\tag{19}
\]

where k is even and each numerator is the exact expression above. All coordinates of the minimizing type attain the minimum. The two limits are 2 and 4 by (10), because D(1-t,t)=O(t^2 log(1/t)) and Phi((1-t)^2,t^2)=O(t^2 log(1/t)). The selector's own ratios diverge, so it remains a valid all-coordinate obstruction.

## 7. Certificate summaries and first unsupported universal line

Illustrative rounded numbers (the saved rational intervals, not these decimals, certify the signs):

| Parameter | X009 min B/I | X009 min Delta/I | B05 min B/I, type | B05 min Delta/I, type |
|---|---:|---:|---|---|
| 4 | 1.620778 | 3.093730 | 1.856782, data | 3.381914, data |
| 6 | 1.895903 | 3.722359 | 2.005170, control | 3.870414, data |
| 8 | 1.973327 | 3.921213 | 2.000454, control | 3.978032, data |
| 10 | 1.993290 | 3.978238 | 2.000037, control | 3.997137, data |
| 12 | 1.998320 | 3.994063 | 2.000003, control | 3.999894, data |
| 14 | 1.999580 | 3.998393 | 2.000000205, control | 4.000000208, control |

Neither known every-coordinate falsifier contradicts existential selection. Also minimizing B_i/I_i need not minimize the actual decrement, even on a fully explicit all-weak-coordinate B05 family. At k=6,8,10,12 a C018-based selection would choose a control, whereas minimizing the exact paid decrement chooses a data bit. The JS term changes the argmin. Thus “the cheapest erasure coordinate minimizes entropy price” is false; the C005 bound only supplies a valid upper charge, not that ordering.

The first unsupported universal line is that every reached nonconstant function under such a policy admits a uniformly paid coordinate, or a terminal reserve paid from the same root influence. Fixing a control to zero produces r=2k-1, outside this stage's fixed r=2k statement; fixing data changes the quadratic phase/dummy pattern and the defect's relative position. Root ordering alone is not a hereditary theorem. Choosing a data/control bit cheaply does not erase the descendant seed entropy or imply the aggregate universal C018 bound B(f)<=K I(f).

# exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

The full target is: there exists one finite absolute C such that for every finite n>=0 and every actual Boolean sign f:{-1,1}^n->{-1,1}, on the uniform cube, H_2(f)=sum_S p_S log2(1/p_S)<=C I(f), where p_S=hat f(S)^2 and I=sum_S |S|p_S=sum_i epsilon_i. Zero masses contribute zero; constants have H=I=0. Natural logarithms are explicitly ln. Full FEI remains OPEN.

This packet consumes the new A098 assignment: audit the quantifiers, global normalizer and conserved influence budget of the higher-level reference after A096's reviewed T12 payment. It resolves a narrower mechanism question, not FEI: a complete spectral reference determined only by the influence vector cannot have universal cross entropy <=K I. Explicit exact obstructions also invalidate the all-degree product-influence reference and two entropy-majorant extensions of the fixed-layer proof. The references here are ideal subprobabilities; failure mass is retained and no integer prefix rounding is claimed.

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

## 1. The complete row, its normalizer, and the one original budget

For a relevant coordinate i, let h_i=(f(+1,x_-i)-f(-1,x_-i))/2. Actual Booleanity gives h_i in {0,+1,-1}, support probability and Fourier energy epsilon_i. Its complete row law is mu_i(T)=p_(T+i)/epsilon_i, T excluding i. Put q_i(T)=p_T+p_(T+i). Both mu_i and q_i are probability laws; q_i is the actual residual spectral marginal, not an unnormalized product prior. It is positive on the support of mu_i. With phi(x,y)=(x+y)h2(y/(x+y)), define

    B=sum_i sum_{T excluding i} phi(p_T,p_(T+i)),
    M_i=sum_T p_(T+i) log2((p_T+p_(T+i))/p_(T+i)).

Then exactly

    M_i=epsilon_i[log2(1/epsilon_i)-D(mu_i||q_i)],
    M<=B<=M+I/ln2,  M=sum_i M_i.                 (1)

The other half of phi is bounded by p_(T+i)/ln2, using ln(1+u)<=u. Terms with p_T=0 or p_(T+i)=0 are handled directly. For I>0, the global joint laws

    P(i,T)=p_(T+i)/I,
    Q(i,T)=(epsilon_i/I)q_i(T)

both have total mass one, and share the coordinate marginal epsilon_i/I. Consequently

    M/I=sum_i (epsilon_i/I)log2(1/epsilon_i)-D(P||Q). (2)

There is exactly one original influence budget I in (2). These are the previously known retained-KL/C018 identities, reconstructed as the audit's acceptance interface, not renamed as new progress. A new reference must specify its complete normalizer and any cost of replacing actual Q. Erasing the divergence, changing Q, or assigning a fresh whole I to every degree is not a paid reduction.

A096 proves T12<=16700/2187 I, supported by a special degree-one row suppression. With the lead's paid root term B_root, the remaining line is

    R_ge2=sum_{|S|>=2} sum_{i notin S} phi(p_S,p_(S+i))<=K I

for one K independent of dimension. One valid sufficient interface would assign nonnegative layer budgets J_m with sum_{m>=2} J_m<=I and prove the level-m bill <=K J_m with one K. Bounds <=K_m I separately at each m do not supply that interface. Equation (2)'s universal lower bound on retained KL is FEI-equivalent up to constants, not a weaker solved lemma.

## 2. Actual Boolean failure of an all-degree sparse-row extension

Let r>=4, delta=2^-r, A_r be the indicator of one control point, and

    F_r=1+A_r(chi_{123}-1)

on r controls and three fresh data coordinates. Every physical influence is delta: data flips change the parity only on activation, and control flips cross the activation point and change the output on half of data inputs. Hence I=(r+3)delta.

The constant coefficient is 1-delta. Other nonzero coefficients have absolute value delta, exactly on nonempty control subsets and on all control subsets united with all three data coordinates. Therefore nonconstant mass W=2delta-delta^2 and

    H=eta((1-delta)^2)+2rW<4I, eta(u)=-u log2 u.   (3)

Indeed eta(u)<= (1-u)/ln2, giving H<=delta(4r+2/ln2)<4(r+3)delta, since ln2>1/2. In particular B<=H<4I; this is no FEI counterfamily.

For a data coordinate h_i=A_r times the parity of the other two data bits. All of its Fourier energy delta is at residual row degrees >=2. Extending A096's degree-one suppression to the whole higher-degree row would assert

    delta<=2delta^2 ln(2/delta).

This fails for every r>=4: the right side is less than 2delta^2(r+1), and 2delta(r+1)<1, starting at 10/16 and decreasing. This is the first invalid inference when linear-form concentration is silently applied to an arbitrary-degree row. Ternary derivative support alone does not produce quadratic suppression of its complete row energy.

## 3. A fully normalized product-influence reference still overcharges

On nonempty subsets consider the natural all-degree extension

    Q_prod(S)=prod_{i in S}epsilon_i/Z,
    Z=prod_i(1+epsilon_i)-1.

It has total mass exactly one. On F_r, Q_prod(S)=delta^|S|/Z and Z=(1+delta)^(r+3)-1>=delta. Its nonempty cross-entropy bill is exactly

    X=sum_{S nonempty}p_S log2(1/Q_prod(S))
      =rI+W log2 Z,
    X/I>=r-2r/(r+3)->infinity.                    (4)

Thus restoring the normalizer does not make this extension influence-paid. For the normalized actual nonempty law p_S/W, Gibbs gives H_nonempty<=X-W log2 W; adding the vacuum entropy still leaves an unpaid divergent reference bill. The complete probability reference with vacuum weight 1/2 and nonempty weights Q_prod/2 has CE=1+X, so adding a vacuum sector does not repair it. A retained KL refund could cancel the excess, but that refund requires proof. Equation (4) refutes this specific reference, not every reference.

## 4. All-degree upper-atom entropy and row-entropy majorants fail

For even n>=2 let b_n=(-1)^(sum_j x_(2j)x_(2j+1)), using physical bits 0,1. Each two-bit block has Fourier coefficients of magnitude 1/2; tensorization proves every full squared coefficient is 2^-n. Consequently

    I=n/2, B=n, H=n,
    sum_S |S|p_S log2(1/p_S)=nI,
    sum_i epsilon_i H(mu_i)=(n-1)I.              (5)

Moreover R_ge2=n-n^2*2^(1-n). Replacing every edge by its upper-atom entropy, as in a layerwise application of phi(x,y)<=y log2(1/y)+y/ln2, leads to the weighted entropy in (5), not H. Neither that term nor the summed complete-row entropies is O(I), even on actual Boolean functions with B/I=2. Fixed-layer conversion is valid, but this all-degree surrogate has destroyed the equal-mass pair cancellation. This diagnostic is distinct from the known fixed-Renyi obstruction.

## 5. Influence-vector-only complete spectral references are impossible

Consider any rule Q_n(epsilon_1,...,epsilon_n) returning a subprobability on all subsets. It may use physical labels and need not be permutation-equivariant. It may also know any permutation-invariant metadata. It may not depend on which Boolean function realizes those same inputs. There is no absolute K making CE(p_f,Q_n(epsilon(f)))<=K I(f) for every actual Boolean sign.

For every integer w>=2, take k=2^w disjoint width-w point conjunctions A_j, n=kw, delta=1/k, and

    f_w=1-2 prod_{j=1}^k(1-A_j).

Every influence equals epsilon=2delta(1-delta)^(k-1), and I=2w(1-delta)^(k-1)<=2w. These equal labeled vectors remain exactly the same under every permutation of the n coordinates. Thus the reference Q is the same for the entire permutation orbit, even for a label-dependent rule.

Average the actual spectral laws over that finite orbit, obtaining bar p. It is uniform conditional on spectral degree m. If Q vanishes on its support, some orbit member has infinite CE. Otherwise linearity and subprobability Gibbs imply

    average_pi CE(p_(f_w o pi),Q)=CE(bar p,Q)
      >=H(bar p)=H(|S|)+E log2 binom(n,|S|).       (6)

This reconstructs A015's exchangeable-location optimum: an exchangeable reference has q_m/binom(n,m), and its infimum is attained at q_m=Pr(|S|=m). Here the orbit/Gibbs argument additionally covers arbitrary nonexchangeable Q that must remain the same for all equal-influence realizations.

The one-block function 1-A_j has constant coefficient 1-delta and every nonempty coefficient of magnitude delta. Therefore the total squared spectral mass of f_w on sets touching exactly one block and having m elements is

    4k delta^2(1-delta)^(2(k-1)) binom(w,m).       (7)

Let a=(1-1/k)^(2(k-1)). Integrating 1/(1-t)<=1/(1-1/k) on [0,1/k] gives ln(1-1/k)>=-1/(k-1), hence a>=e^-2>1/9. The elementary exponential series gives e<3. At least 2^(w-1) subsets of a block have m>=ceil(w/2), so (7) gives at least 2/9 spectral mass with m in [ceil(w/2),w]. On these degrees

    binom(n,m)=prod_{j=0}^{m-1}(n-j)/(m-j)
      >=(n/m)^m>=k^m,
    log2 binom(n,m)>=wm>=w^2/2.

Consequently H(bar p)>=w^2/9. Some coordinate permutation pi therefore satisfies

    CE(p_(f_w o pi),Q)/I(f_w)>=w/18.             (8)

This proves the all-parameter no-go, with no asymptotic extrapolation from a census and no hypothesis of exchangeability imposed on Q itself. It strengthens A015's obstruction by allowing influence-aware rules: all influences here are equal, so their vector provides no block-location information. It is a coding-cost obstruction, not a lower bound on the original H(f_w) and not a disproof of FEI. It does not exclude function-adapted references, signed-coefficient information, adaptive histories, references with a paid KL correction, or references directly coding conditional edges instead of the entire spectrum.

## 6. Honest first unsupported universal line

The complete row normalizer and joint influence budget are known, but the needed all-degree retained-KL payment in (2), equivalently R_ge2<=K I after paid layers, is unproved. Sparse support alone, a product-influence prior, atom entropy majorization and any complete spectral prior depending only on the influence vector are now ruled out as sufficient payments. The next construction must retain function-specific spectral/support information and cancellation between the two endpoint masses. Nothing here proves that such a construction cannot exist.

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

## 1. Acceptance interface and independent A096 reconstruction

For every relevant i, write e_i=Inf_i, mu_i(T)=p_(T+i)/e_i and r_i(T)=p_T+p_(T+i), T excluding i. Both are complete probability laws. Define D_i=D(mu_i||r_i), M_i=sum_T p_(T+i)log2(r_i(T)/p_(T+i)), M=sum_i M_i. Then

    M=sum_i e_i log2(1/e_i)-sum_i e_i D_i,
    M<=B<=M+I/ln2.

The joint laws P(i,T)=p_(T+i)/I and Q(i,T)=(e_i/I)r_i(T) normalize to one and share coordinate marginal e_i/I. Their joint divergence is I^-1 sum_i e_i D_i. These previously established identities are the exact interface, not new progress.

Take h=chi_Z product_{b=1}^m f_b, with ell>=2 fresh parity bits and arbitrary finite Boolean seed f. Let J=mI(f)+ell, F=1+A_k(h-1), q=2^-k and d=(1-q)^2+q^2. The row laws for a seed coordinate in h are the seed row laws tensored with the SAME spectator spectral distribution; their likelihood ratio is unchanged. Parity rows have mu=r and D=0. Hence original row divergences equal D_i(f), and the summed seed contribution is mI(f)D_f.

On activation, all core-support rows are extended by a uniform control-subset law. On derivative support the complete reference is q times that extension; the vacuum exception is outside it because h has no singleton coefficients. Thus each relevant core row satisfies D_i(F)=D_i(h)+k. A control row has exceptional derivative probability q, complete reference probability d, and all other occupied likelihood ratios 1/(2q), of total derivative mass 1-q. Therefore

    D_c=(1-q)(k-1)+q log2(q/d),
    I(F)=q(J+k),
    I(F)D_F=q[mI(f)D_f+kJ+kD_c],
    M(F)=qmM(f)+k[q(1-q)+q^2 log2(d/q^2)].       (1)

Only the k-bit activation rarity is universally refunded. The arbitrary seed divergence is still present. In the lower-endpoint degree>=2 tail, all positive core costs lie above the parity support. A control contributes q/2 from core sets and q^2[2^(k-1)-k] from pure-control sets, giving

    M_ge2(F)=qmM(f)+kq(1-kq).                   (2)

For a general fixed ell, replace the pure-control count by sum_{j=ell}^{k-1} binom(k-1,j). These reconstruct the finite part of A096@1, including constants (their seed contribution is zero) and irrelevant coordinates (omitted).

The cutoff quantifiers also hold. For nonconstant seed dimension n>=1 and finite nonnegative s(N)=o(N), eta_m=sup_{N>=mn}s(N)/N is finite and tends to zero. Choose k_m=ceil[4(n+1)m sqrt(eta_m)+2sqrt(m)]. Then k_m=o(m), N_m=mn+ell+k_m<=(n+2)m eventually, and s(N_m)<=4(n+1)m sqrt(eta_m). Eventually k_m-s(N_m)>log2(mI(f)+ell+k_m), so the actual F_m meets I(F_m)<=2^-s(N_m). Its retained tail contains qmM(f), so its ratio is at least mM(f)/(mI(f)+ell+k_m); this lower bound tends to M(f)/I(f). The reverse supremum inequality is M_geell<=M. For an infinite unrestricted supremum, apply this argument to each finite seed ratio, rather than exchange supremum and limit. This independently accepts A096-TERMINAL-KL-REFUND-AUDIT@1 at its full original scope without contributing a repair. A073's existing independent acceptance is consumed and retained.

## 2. Fixed bounded-degree inspection preserves normalizers but loses refund

Fix an integer L>=0. Let C_L keep each residual Fourier atom T with |T|<=L individually, and merge all |T|>L into one tail atom. Define

    D_i^L=D(C_L mu_i || C_L r_i),
    M^L=sum_i e_i[log2(1/e_i)-D_i^L].

The complete reference is coarsened, never conditioned on derivative support. Thus its normalizer remains exactly one. Data processing implies D_i^L<=D_i and M^L>=M>=0. Exact KL chain rule gives

    M^L-M=sum_i e_i mu_i(tail)
               D(mu_i(.|tail)||r_i(.|tail)).     (3)

Singleton cells have no conditional KL. Equation (3) identifies precisely the missing currency: within-tail likelihood ratios. Merely counting tail mass does not retain them.

## 3. Full-support, vanishing-influence witness with no auxiliary factor

Let f_n be the n-bit sign that is -1 at one point and +1 elsewhere, n>=2. Put e=2^(1-n). Its constant coefficient is 1-e and every nonempty coefficient has magnitude e. Every physical influence equals e, so I(f_n)=ne. Every Fourier coefficient is nonzero. Its oriented row bill is exactly

    M_i=e-e^2+e^2 log2(((1-e)^2+e^2)/e^2),
    M(f_n)/I(f_n)<=1+2(n-1)e<=2.                (4)

The last inequalities use (1-e)^2+e^2<=1 and (n-1)2^(1-n)<=1/2. Also, writing eta(u)=-u log2 u and W=2e-e^2,

    H(f_n)=eta((1-e)^2)+2(n-1)W
       <=2e/ln2+4(n-1)e<4ne,                   (5)

since eta(u)<=(1-u)/ln2 and ln2>1/2.

For fixed L take n>=max(6,2L+2), m=2^floor(n/2) and G_n=f_n^tensor(m). All mn coordinates remain relevant, every influence equals e, every Fourier coefficient remains nonzero, and there are no auxiliary parity bits or dummy coordinates. Tensor product spectra prove H and I add, while each derivative likelihood ratio has the same independent spectator factor in numerator and denominator. Thus

    I(G_n)=mn e=n*2^(1-ceil(n/2))->0,
    H(G_n)/I(G_n)<4, M(G_n)/I(G_n)<=2.           (6)

We now bound the coarsened divergence in every row. Write the residual subset as its seed-block component and m-1 spectator-block components. The seed derivative law is uniform on its 2^(n-1) residual atoms: each has probability e. Therefore total derivative mass on the individually inspected low atoms is at most

    beta=e sum_{j=0}^L binom(n-1,j).

On any occupied row atom, mu_i/r_i<=1/e because r_i>=p_(T+i). Thus the contribution of all low singleton cells to D_i^L is at most beta(n-1), including any negative terms.

Set a=(1-e)^2. The complete reference tail has probability at least the event that exactly one spectator block has degree >L and all other spectators are empty. The seed row is unrestricted and its reference mass is one. This event has mass

    (m-1) z a^(m-2),
    z=e^2 sum_{j=L+1}^n binom(n,j).

For n>=2L+2, binomial symmetry gives z>=e. For n>=6, me<=1/4 and m-1>=m/2. Bernoulli's inequality gives a^(m-2)>=(1-2e)^(m-2)>=1-2e(m-2)>=1/2. Consequently

    r_i(tail)>=me/4=2^-(ceil(n/2)+1).

The tail KL term mu_tail log2(mu_tail/r_tail) is at most log2(1/r_tail), since mu_tail<=1; this remains true when the term is negative. Hence

    D_i^L<=beta(n-1)+ceil(n/2)+1,
    M^L(G_n)/I(G_n)>=floor(n/2)-2-beta(n-1).     (7)

For fixed L, beta(n-1) is an exponentially decaying polynomial in n, so (7) diverges. Together with (3),(6), it also proves

    (M^L-M)/I>=floor(n/2)-4-beta(n-1)->infinity. (8)

This is a normalized-reference obstruction on actual Boolean functions even at vanishing total influence and full Fourier support. It is stronger than the parity-shift diagnostic below because stripping dummy coordinates or an independent parity factor does not remove it.

The same witness has an exact repair if its genuine block structure is retained: discard only the independent spectator components, keeping the complete seed row. The likelihood ratio is then unchanged and KL loss is zero. M<=2I is still paid from the one original influence budget by (4). This is a comparison of two quotients on the same family, not a new class-FEI theorem. Absolute degree merging and spectator-aware marginalization are mathematically different operations.

## 4. Exact activated-row mutation and scope boundary

An additional audit on A096's precise interface takes h=chi_Z f_n^tensor(m), ell=L+2, F=1+A_k(h-1). Every core derivative row has residual degree >L, and its coarsened derivative is concentrated on the tail. Direct complete-reference counting gives

    r_i(tail)=q+q^2 sum_{j=L+1}^k binom(k,j),
    D_i^L=log2(1/r_i(tail))<=k.                 (9)

Taking m=2^n and k=n, seed rows alone give M^L/I>=2n(n-1)/(3n+ell), while actual M/I<=2. Actual H/I<4 follows from (5), H(h)=mH(f_n), and the balanced activation spectrum, whose selector entropy is <=4kq+2q/ln2 and whose physical influence is q(mI(f_n)+ell+k). The full-support tensor witness in (7) already suffices; (9) supplies a literal mutation of the accepted A096 normalizer/refund.

The conclusion excludes exactly fixed bounded-degree atom inspection followed by one undifferentiated tail, and any weaker partition obtained by merging its cells. It does not exclude an adaptive degree bound growing with the function, a structured tail reference, the full row KL, or a signed conditional reference that preserves tail information. A031's earlier carrier-only scalar-envelope obstruction is not repeated or upgraded; this proof concerns an explicitly normalized KL coarsening and its exact within-tail loss.

## 5. First unsupported universal implication

No dimension-independent bound has been proved for the complete terminal bill M, or for R_ge2 after the already paid root and T12 layers. The surviving line is

    D(P_f||Q_f)>=sum_i(e_i/I)log2(1/e_i)-K

for one finite K and every actual Boolean seed. Replacing D by the normalized D^L gives a strictly stronger sufficient requirement; (7) refutes that replacement. The large exact activation refund alone does not imply the surviving line. A signed Booleanity argument must retain or pay the within-tail conditional divergence, not infer payment from tail probability or a finite degree prefix.

# exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Full FEI asks for one finite absolute C such that every finite n>=0 and every actual Boolean sign on the uniform {-1,1}^n cube satisfies H_2(f)<=C I(f). Here p_S=hat f(S)^2, H_2=sum_S p_S log2(1/p_S), I=sum_S |S|p_S; zero masses vanish and constants have H=I=0. Full FEI remains OPEN.

This stage audits terminal ownership of the newly reviewed positive-erasure binary quotient. It closes one explicit shared-core mechanism: for all even d>=2 and all r>=1, an informative four-cell quotient pays both its retained selector price and discarded conditional KL from one original root influence partition. Its enlarged audit bill is <=(7/2)I. A binary-only quotient, with all its losses assigned to a single core coordinate, fails at every fixed rate when r=2^d. These are scoped root-reference results, not a universal theorem or a hereditary stopping-tree payment. Entropy and KL are in bits.

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

## 1. Source scope and the new ownership question

For every relevant i use complete laws mu_i(T)=p_(T+i)/e_i and r_i(T)=p_T+p_(T+i), e_i=Inf_i. For a row quotient C define D_i^C=D(Cmu_i||Cr_i), M_i^C=e_i[log2(1/e_i)-D_i^C], and L_i^C=e_i[D(mu_i||r_i)-D_i^C]. Then M_i^C=M_i+L_i^C, with the exact lost conditional KL retained. M<=B<=M+I/ln2 is the existing interface.

A073@1 proves a binary remainder bound for ONE two-bit point-edited bent gate. Its reserve is dedicated to only those two losses. A098 independently accepts that full original scope. We consume this acceptance; no inference licenses repeating that reserve across arbitrarily many selector pairs. The new test keeps the same bent core and lets many pair gates share its one marked vertex.

## 2. Actual Boolean shared-core family and full spectrum

Let d>=2 be even, delta=2^-d and t=2^(-d/2). Let b(y)=(-1)^(sum_j y_(2j-1)y_(2j)) on 0/1 cube labels. Its Fourier amplitudes are sigma_T t, with signs sigma_T in {+1,-1}, counts N_+=(2^d+2^(d/2))/2 and N_-=(2^d-2^(d/2))/2, proved by two-bit tensor factors and b(0)=1.

Let q_j(u_j,v_j) be -1 only at (1,1), with Fourier coefficients (1,1,1,-1)/2. On disjoint selector pairs put Q_r=product_j q_j, alpha=2^-r, and

    F_(d,r)=b(y)+1_(y=0)(Q_r-1).

It equals b away from the marked vertex and equals Q_r there, so is an actual Boolean sign. Its zero-selector coefficients are sigma_T t+delta(alpha-1); every nonzero-selector coefficient has magnitude delta alpha, with sign inherited from Q_r. All coefficients are nonzero, hence every selector row has positive erasure. Physical edge counting gives

    e=Inf_(u_j)=Inf_(v_j)=delta/2,
    a=Inf_(y_l)=1/2+delta(1-alpha),
    I_core=da, J=I_selector=r delta, I=da+r delta. (1)

Indeed Q_r has mean alpha and each of its bit influences is 1/2. Each core zero-to-neighbor edge under b was nonpivotal; modifying the marked value adds 2delta Pr(Q_r=-1)=delta(1-alpha) influence. All influences are ordinary physical ones.

## 3. Exact binary loss and its single-owner obstruction

Fix a selector query, its paired partner occupancy V, and the subset R of all other selector pairs. Write E for the event R is empty, and

    w=Pr_mu(E)=4^(1-r)=4alpha^2,
    A=1-delta+2delta alpha^2,
    R_sigma=1+2sigma t(alpha-1)
                 +delta[(alpha-1)^2+alpha^2].

The derivative law is uniform over all residual subsets. Retaining V alone gives mu_V=(1/2,1/2), r_V=(1-e,e), exactly as for the original r=1 gate. But its conditional remainder has changed. Define the normalized core divergence

    D_y=sum_sigma N_sigma delta log2(A/R_sigma).

On partner-one the conditional laws agree. On partner-zero, the derivative gives E mass w, while the complete conditional reference gives E mass z=A/(1-e) and complement mass e(1-w)/(1-e). Conditional on nonempty R the laws agree; conditional on E the core divergence is D_y. Hence

    D0=D((w,1-w)||(z,1-z))+w D_y,
    D_full=D_V+D0/2, L_i^V=e D0/2.              (2)

For r>=2, w<=1/4. By binary KL expansion, dropping the nonnegative w log2(1/z) term, using h2(w)<=1, and (1-e)/e>=2^d,

    D0>=(1-w)log2((1-e)/(e(1-w)))-h2(w)
       >=3d/4-1.

There are 2r selector queries. For r=2^d their total binary loss is at least 3d/8-1/2, whereas any one specified core influence a is at most 3/4. Thus no absolute K pays all these binary losses by K times that single core influence. This refutes the extrapolated shared-owner shortcut, not A073's expressly r=1 reserve. A bounded alphabet alone neither bounds conditional KL nor supplies unused terminal capacity.

## 4. Four cells retain the missing spectator likelihood ratio

Instead retain both V and the empty/nonempty status of R. In order (V=0,E), (V=0,not E), (V=1,E), (V=1,not E), the complete laws are

    mu_C=(w/2,(1-w)/2,w/2,(1-w)/2),
    r_C=(A,e(1-w),ew,e(1-w)).                   (3)

Both sum to one, including reference mass outside derivative-heavy cells. At r=1 the zero-mass nonempty cells are omitted continuously. Only the (V=0,E) cell has residual conditional KL. Therefore

    D_full-D_C=w D_y/2,
    L_i^C=e w D_y/2,
    sum_selector L_i^C=r delta w D_y/2.          (4)

The core conditionals have likelihood ratios A/R_sigma. They are bounded by 3 because R_+ is the smaller denominator and

    3R_+-A=2(1-t)(1-2t)
              +6t(1-t)alpha+4t^2 alpha^2>=0.

Here t<=1/2 and alpha<=1/2. Thus 0<=D_y<=log2 3<2. Also rw<=1: r=1 attains one and subsequent ratios (r+1)/(4r)<1. Consequently the ENTIRE four-cell loss is <delta<=a/2 for all d,r. Half of one named core coordinate can pay these losses once. This first bound alone is a remainder-only ledger; the retained price must still be accounted for.

## 5. Retained price and remainder are paid together from one root partition

For the four cells in (3), exact likelihood algebra cancels the residual core distribution and gives

    M_i^C/e=1+(w/2)[d+2r-1+log2 A].             (5)

In particular the r=1 retained local price still grows linearly in d; it cannot simply be discarded after certifying a small L_i. Since A<=1, rw<=1 and r^2w<=1,

    sum_selector M_i^C-J<=delta(d/2+1).

For r^2w the values at r=1,2 equal one, and ratios ((r+1)/r)^2/4 decrease below one thereafter. Add ALL losses in (4), obtaining the total extra charge

    X=sum_selector(M_i^C+L_i^C)-J
      <delta(d/2+2)<=3/4<=(3/2)a.               (6)

The middle bound follows at d=2 and decreases as even d increases. The owner in (6) is the single specified physical coordinate y_1, not a fresh copy of its influence for each query. No retained price or discarded remainder uses any other fraction of that owner.

Each core coordinate has a>=1/2. The generic conditional entropy bound gives B_(y_l)<=h2(a)<=1<=2a. For selectors B_i<=M_i+e/ln2<=M_i^C+e/ln2. Thus even the enlarged bill that ALSO explicitly budgets every L_i^C is bounded by

    B(F)<=T:=2I_core+sum_selector M_i^C
                       +sum_selector L_i^C+J/ln2
         <=(7/2)I_core+(5/2)J<=(7/2)I.          (7)

We used ln2>2/3, obtained from the first positive atanh term. Its ownership ledger is concrete: y_1 has baseline 2a plus at most (3/2)a extra; every other core coordinate has baseline 2a; selector coordinates have baseline (1+1/ln2)e<(5/2)e. One original rate-7/2 influence partition pays all of T. The unused root row quota above baseline on y_1 is sufficient. No recursive terminal-child entropy surplus or hereditary availability is inferred from this root quota.

This differs from merely proving another bounded-ratio class: it resolves the actual quotient-loss and repeated-owner mechanism that failed in (2), accounts for the retained price explicitly, and provides exact acceptance conditions for the next conditional row reference.

## 6. Paid physical scale and the first unproved universal line

This is no FEI counterfamily. Zero-selector squared coefficients are at least (t-delta)^2>=delta/4, so their entropy contribution is <=d+2. The remaining selector sector has mass delta(1-alpha^2), with atom log2(1/p)=2d+2r. Hence

    H(F)<=d+2+2d delta+2r delta<=4I(F),

using d>=2 and 1-alpha>=1/2. The direct bound is supplementary; it is not used to pay the quotient or assign its owner.

For arbitrary non-flat cores the signed ratios A/R_sigma need not satisfy the bound above, and there need not be a core coordinate with influence at least 1/2. After a restriction, the named root row quota may also have been spent. The first unsupported universal step is to produce an actual signed conditional partition AND an available certified child/terminal reserve for its retained bill and remainder, with no reuse. Equivalently the full C018 line sum_i e_i D_i>=sum_i e_i log2(1/e_i)-K I remains open for one absolute K. This stage proves neither that line nor a stopping-tree policy.

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

The TeX ledger proves all statements by Fourier expansion, injective support maps, entropy chain rule, a finite union bound and a proved finite-law continuity bound. The critical exact smoothed ledger, absent from A001's independent reconstruction, is as follows. Fix a balanced diffuse seed b on n bits, a balanced gadget g with entropy H_g and influence I_g, and m>=2. Point-activate b^tensor m using s=ceil(2 log2 m), p=2^-s; systematically code its N=mn+s coordinates with r auxiliary bits. Write e_j=Pr((AS)_j=1) for those auxiliary spectral memberships and Q=sp+sum e_j. Replace only the s controls and r auxiliary physical bits by disjoint g gadgets. The resulting dimension is mn+d_g(s+r) and

    H(F) = pm H(b) + 2sp(2-p) + phi((1-p)^2) + H_g Q,
    I(F) = pm I(b) + I_g Q,
    EF = 1-p, Var(F)=p(2-p), 0<=Q<=p[s+r(2-p)].

Original data influences are p I_i(b). Every gadget leaf is charged by its host influence times its own leaf influence. Thus the code's potentially variance-sized influence is diluted rather than discarded. Physical inputs are independent; the new spectral memberships can be deterministic parity functions of old memberships.

Choose a systematic code with distance >2k+1 and a majority-of-parities gadget with minimum nonempty support 2k+1 and internal distance >=4k+2. Different outer supports differ on enough occupied blocks; different terms within one outer support differ inside a gadget. This proves distance >2k. Removing <=k physical coordinates cannot collide supports, preserves entropy and mean and subtracts exactly the queried influences. The children remain numerically in the same epsilon class with distance >k. This is a finite-depth plateau, not arbitrary restriction closure. All error terms are O_g(p log m), while the principal H/I terms are pm times the fixed seed values. This independently verifies R01 and, with activation length o(m) chosen explicitly in the TeX ledger, R02 from A001's graph.

The two-cell test uses the actual arbitrary seed: g_m=z f^tensor m and M_g=((1+g)/2)y1+((1-g)/2)y2. A class bound C gives mH(f)+4<=C[mI(f)+3]; m->infinity recovers H(f)<=C I(f). Point activation with s=ceil(2 log2(m+1)) has influence and minority probability tending to zero and ratio tending to the same seed ratio. The converse for both optimal constants is subset inclusion.

The packet's full-support test is addressed in both possible senses. If it means all physical variables relevant, delete irrelevant coordinates from the separated-support construction; all remaining ledgers survive. If it means every Fourier coefficient nonzero, exact separation is incompatible with it. For this second endpoint take the elementary activated tensor G_m on D_m=mn+1+s bits, append two dummy bits, and flip one physical value. With M_m=D_m+2 and delta=2^-M_m, coefficients change by exactly +/-2delta. Old nonzero coefficients have magnitude >=2^-D_m, so none can cancel; old zeros all become nonzero. Each added or old coordinate costs at most 2delta in influence, and total influence error is at most 2M_m delta.

The squared-spectrum total variation is at most 2sqrt(delta), by Parseval and Cauchy-Schwarz. A maximal-coupling proof bounds entropy change by h2(tau)+tau log2(2^M_m-1). These errors are exponentially small compared with the activated tensor influence of order 1/m. Hence all-Fourier-support plus arbitrarily small influence and minority probability again has optimal constant C_*. With slack, the same surgery preserves the relative-influence conditions of the smoothed construction; it destroys exact distance and plateau. No numerical logarithmic approximation is used to prove this limit.

For the auxiliary endpoint, an actual fixed base F_*(X,Y) times arbitrary fresh-data f^tensor m preserves its X spectral marginal and X-degree. Its conditional entropy adds mH(f) and its influence adds mI(f). Dividing any proposed dimension-free conditional bound by m proves full FEI. A hypothesis on the entire conditional spectrum, such as genuine product conditional laws, generally fails under this embedding when the arbitrary seed spectrum is nonproduct; it is not a free preserved hypothesis.

First unsupported line for a universal FEI conclusion remains: a finite bound on the entropy/influence ratios of any one of the admitted arbitrary-seed classes. The ledgers cannot supply that bound by renaming it. No unbounded Boolean H/I family has been found.

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

For probabilities x,y, let E(x,y)=phi(x)+phi(y)-phi(x+y), phi(x)=-x log2 x. B_i is the sum of E along coordinate-i spectral edges. Homogeneity supplies the new ledger. Disjoint tensors add B; fresh balancing preserves B; character multiplication and dummy padding preserve B. A systematic parity code has B=sum_(columns zero) B_i(seed); every auxiliary B_i is zero. Parity lift and all distance>1 coded constructions have B=0.

General point activation of h with mean a, d=1-a, controls s, p=2^-s, c=(1-pd)^2 and q_i=hat h({i})^2 gives

    B_data,i = p[B_i(h)-E(a^2,q_i)] + p(1-p)E(d^2,q_i)+E(c,p^2q_i),
    B_control = p(1-a^2)+(p-2p^2)d^2+E(c,p^2d^2).

The proof partitions all spectral edges into nonempty retained-data edges, nonempty control edges at empty data, and the exceptional constant edge. For a balanced b^tensor m, m>=2, singleton coefficients vanish, hence

    B(activation)=pmB(b)+s[2p-2p^2+E((1-p)^2,p^2)].

With economical s=ceil(2log2 m), B/H tends to B(b)/H(b), preserving rather than inflating the seed fraction. Control-dominated activation has limiting fraction 1/2. The selector formula is

    B(M_g)=B(g)/2 + (1/2)sum_i E(1,hat g({i})^2).

For g=z f^tensor m only the singleton {z} is possible, of mass (Ef)^(2m), so B(M_g)=mB(f)/2+E(1,(Ef)^(2m))/2. Therefore its fraction also tends to the seed's. The labels have zero erasure because each spectral set contains exactly one leaf label. These are aggregate-B conclusions beyond the retired H/I ledgers.

The strongest direct mixing repair tested is adding a fresh bent factor. For F=f^tensor m times bent b_r on 2r variables,

    H=mH(f)+2r, B=mB(f)+2r, I=mI(f)+r, D=mD(f).

Let R=H(f)/I(f), eta=D(f)/H(f)>0 and epsilon_F=D(F)/H(F). Then exactly

    H(F)/I(F) = 2/[1+(2/R-1)epsilon_F/eta].

Forcing B/H->1 forces ratio ->2 and cannot preserve a fixed seed ratio >2. More generally, tensoring any auxiliary with H/I<=C cannot preserve a seed ratio >C in that limit. This is an obstruction to this specified mechanism, not to every Boolean admission map. For seed ratios <=2, paid bent dilution plus a tuned deterministic parity does achieve both requested limits, and keeps a physical seed tensor. It pays dominant entropy/influence overhead; the unsolved case begins with ratios >2. The three-bit one-point defect is an exact such seed, with H=4-(9/8)log2 3, I=3/4 and D=1-(15/8)log2 5+(18/8)log2 3>0. Integer inequalities certify both H>2I and D>0.

Exact B=H implies a product spectral law, and Boolean dyadic rigidity then forces marginals in {0,1/2,1}, a character times a bent sign on an even coordinate subset, and H<=2I. This source-complete endpoint is already proved in ERASURE_RIGIDITY section 1. It does not extend quantitatively merely by continuity.

Indeed a concrete Boolean family is c_t times b_r, where c_t=(-1)^(uv) and u,v are parities of disjoint t-bit blocks, t>=2. Its dimension is 2t+2r, mean 2^(-(r+1)), and

    H=2+2r, B=2r, D=2, I=t+r,
    TC=2t-2, TV(P,product_i P_i)=1-2^(2-2t).

Take t=r^2, r>=2. Then B/H->1 but TC/H->infinity and TV->1. Thus near-one erasure does not force dimension-independent closeness to the product of spectral marginals, even for actual Boolean spectra. This low-ratio family pays its dependence with ample physical influence; it is not a counterexample to FEI or to a near-erasure H/I theorem.

Finally, if one fixed epsilon in (0,1) admits a bound H<=C_epsilon I on B>=(1-epsilon)H, then full FEI follows with constant **max(C_epsilon,2/(epsilon ln2))**, improving the additive C_epsilon+2/(epsilon ln2) inference from the erasure-fraction endpoint. Proof: a minimal-variable violator at C has all physical children certified. For delta=H-CI>0, every coordinate gives B_i>=(C-2/ln2)I_i+delta. Summation gives D<(2/ln2)I. Since its ratio exceeds C>=2/(epsilon ln2), it lies in the near-one class, contradiction. Constants and irrelevant coordinates are handled explicitly. The proof retains child deficits rather than assuming the near-one class is restriction closed.

First unsupported line: a fixed-epsilon, dimension-independent H/I bound on the near-one class, or a different actual Boolean transformation that inflates B/H without diluting a seed ratio above two. No such high-ratio admission or quantitative Boolean stability estimate is proved. This honest gap is the continuing universal target; no finite counterexample to a candidate constant is labeled a disproof of FEI.

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

GROWING_BLOCK_PROOF.md sections1-7 is complete. A deterministic greedy parity-check construction with r=ceil(2b log2(2D)) tags gives spectral distance>b when b log D=o(D). It preserves entropy and spends at most r Var(g) auxiliary influence. Each physical restriction through b bits is injective on the old support and retains full old entropy.

For one physical edit, a size-a row with old amplitude c has exact collision

    (2^a-1)(2p)^2(2^a*2p-2eta*c)^2
        /(c^2-2eta*c*2p+2^a*(2p)^2), p=2^-N.

Weighted Cauchy bounds this by2^(2a)(2p)^2 per row. Hence C_A<=2^(a+2)p and TOTAL collision through block size b is <=4b(2N)^b2^-N. This is the new arbitrary/growing-block extension; no new higher-arity refund identity is assumed.

These actual roots have full Fourier support, no nontrivial disjoint physical tensor factor, I and max influence->0, mean->1, and H/I->the arbitrary seed ratio. Total collision over ALL blocks through b_t tends to0 with b_t->infinity; total pair refunds also tends to0. The restricted root class with the SINGLE seed-independent cutoff q(N)=max(1,floor(cube_root(N))) and fixed positive rarity/mean/collision thresholds therefore retains the universal optimal extended-real FEI constant without factor loss. This follows because b_t/q(N_t)->infinity for each fixed seed dimension. The finite N12470 control certifies only b4, not this eventual cube-root scale.

# exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

For every n>=0 and Boolean sign f on the uniform cube, put a_S=fhat(S), p_S=a_S^2, H=sum p_S log2(1/p_S), I=sum |S|p_S and alpha_i=sum_{S containing i}p_S. All entropy and coding costs are in bits. Terms at zero atoms are zero. Constants have H=I=0 and need no selected coordinate. All universal constants must be finite and independent of n,f and representation. Let

    B_i=H(S_i|S_-i), B=sum_i B_i,
    L_i=sum_{T not containing i, p_(T+i)>0} p_(T+i) log2(1+p_T/p_(T+i)),
    L=sum_i L_i, a=2/ln2, b=1/ln2.

This L is the downward signed-Booleanity route's logarithmic target, not the B05 mismatch quantity also named L. Signed admission means ALL constraints sum_S a_S^2=1 and sum_S a_S a_(S xor U)=0 for every U!=empty, with real coefficients.

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

## 1. Explicit implication diagram and constants

For each node below, quantify over ALL finite Boolean cubes. E_K means B<=KI. G_K means each nonconstant f has a relevant i with B_i<=K alpha_i. D_C means each nonconstant f has a relevant i with Delta_i<=C alpha_i. S_K means L<=KI under full signed admission. SG_K means each nonconstant f has a relevant i with L_i<=K alpha_i. J_K means for every f there exists one admissible representation, one globally feasible positive lambda, and distinct-run posteriors with complete residual R<=KI. There is no change of representation per individual spectral coordinate.

```mermaid
flowchart TD
 FEI[FEI C] -->|B <= H: K=C| E[Aggregate erasure E_K]
 E -->|sum over relevant coordinates: same K| G[Good coordinate G_K]
 G -->|JS paid: C=K+2/ln2| D[Entropy decrement D_C]
 D -->|dimension induction: same C| FEI
 E -->|L <= B: same K| S[Full signed downward residual S_K]
 S -->|B <= L+I/ln2: K grows by 1/ln2| E
 S -->|sum: same K| SG[Good downward coordinate SG_K]
 SG -->|upward + JS: C=K+3/ln2| D
 J[Complete posterior witness J_K] -->|C=2+Kref+K| FEI
 T[Stopping-tree erasure K; leaf FEI C0] -->|C=max K+2/ln2,C0| FEI
```

Here Kref=3+2log2(3)+log2(6e). FEI(C) also directly implies S_C and SG_C. FEI(C) implies G_C via E_C. G_K implies E_(K+a) via FEI; SG_K implies S_(K+3b) via FEI. No same-constant reverse arrow FEI(C)->D_C or FEI(C)->J_K is proved. The general block analogue retains H(S_A|S_Ac), not sum of child erasures, and queries actual physical coordinates. Choosing all coordinates merely restates FEI and supplies no smaller reduction.

Proof of the arrows: conditioning and the entropy chain rule give B<=H. For each edge (T,T+i), binary entropy gives

    B_i=L_i+sum_T p_T log2(1+p_(T+i)/p_T).

The second sum is between zero and alpha_i/ln2 because ln(1+x)<=x, including zero endpoints by continuity. Consequently L<=B<=L+bI. Summing inequalities weighted by positive alpha_i gives existence of the required coordinate; irrelevant coordinates have zero B_i,L_i,Delta_i. Actual Boolean restrictions give Delta_i=B_i+JS_i with 0<=JS_i<=a alpha_i. Finally I=alpha_i+(I(f_+)+I(f_-))/2 makes dimension induction exact, with constant functions as the base case. Full signed admission is equivalent to Booleanity: inverse Walsh transforming the convolution equations gives f(x)^2=1 at every point. Thus the signed vector target has exactly the same domain, not a relaxation to positive laws.

The tree arrow follows by telescoping H and I over a finite adaptive restriction tree. If q_v are actual reach probabilities, cumulative erasure <=K sum_v q_v alpha_(i_v)(f_v) and each leaf H<=C0 I imply H(root)<=max(K+a,C0)I(root). A terminal class defined only by B=0 is inadmissible: parity lifts preserve arbitrary seed entropy. One must retain its full terminal H. This is C019's precise remaining payment.

## 2. Exact minimal posterior and the extra coding debt

Freeze an admissible gate union and ordered covers from forests.tex, its feasible lambda and the finite set W_A of distinct actual partition-forest runs for each nonempty spectral A. Let B_r=B_lambda(A;r), c=Lambda+log2 Z, and t_A=sum_{r in W_A}2^(-B_r). All these t_A are positive on spectral support. They are not asserted to be probabilities. The Gibbs variational identity gives exactly

    min_P [E_P B_r-H(P)] = -log2 t_A,
    P_A^*(r)=2^(-B_r)/t_A.

Proof: E B-H(P)=-log2 t_A+D(P||P_A^*). Distinctness is essential; counting multiple descriptions of one actual run would invalidate the mass bound. Define the fully optimized residual

    R_* = v c + sum_{A!=empty} p_A[-log2 t_A],  v=1-p_empty.

For each run the recovered proof gives q(r)>=2^(-c-Kref|A|-B_r), where q is the root-conditioned actual queue law. Hence

    U(A)=2^(-c-Kref|A|)t_A <= Q_*(A), sum_A U(A)<=1.

U is a strictly positive subprobability on spectral support. It may assign mass to nonspectral nonempty sets too. For v>0 set P^+(A)=p_A/v. The generalized divergence D(P^+||U)=sum_A P^+(A)log2(P^+(A)/U(A)) is nonnegative: if m=sum U, it equals D(P^+||U/m)-log2 m. Direct substitution, with no inequality, proves

    h2(v)+Kref I+R_* = H(f)+v D(P^+||U).             (A003.1)

The v=0 case is declared trivial separately; no P^+ is defined there. This identity is a sharpened comparison of the routes. A fixed witness reference asks to pay exactly an additional coding-divergence debt. Discarding posterior entropy cannot help: every other posterior has residual at least R_*. Retaining only its reference normalization cannot help either: it leaves the shape divergence D(P^+||U/m) unpaid.

Thus J_K implies FEI with C=2+Kref+K using h2(v)<=2I, but FEI alone does not establish J_K for this restricted coding family. A sufficient reverse admission lemma, conditional on FEI(C), is: for every f choose an admissible reference with vD(P^+||U)<=d I, with one finite d; then R_*<=(C+d-Kref)I, and certainly R_*<=(C+d)I. This is a conditional inference, not a claim that d is bounded or that reverse implication is false. For unrestricted references U=P^+, the debt vanishes, but their admission into the actual forest family is precisely unproved.

The weakest exact certificate within the frozen forest upper-bound method for a specified final C is

    exists admissible choices: R_* <= (C-Kref)I-h2(v).

The campaign's R_*<=KI is a convenient slightly stronger payment because it pays h2(v) separately. It cannot be replaced by a per-coordinate forest cost or a posterior on repeated descriptions.

## 3. Smallest additional inequalities for the three routes

| Route | Already proved interface | First additional inequality, with exact scope | Why materially stronger than the interface |
|---|---|---|---|
| Witness C020 | Complete variational forest bound and (A003.1) | One finite K, every Boolean f, one global admissible representation/lambda with R_*<=KI; or the exact C-certificate above | Finite covers, queue normalization and optimal posteriors provide no uniform expected payment. The full weighted divergence remains. |
| Erasure C018 | Delta_i=B_i+JS_i and the exact influence decrement | One finite K, every nonconstant Boolean f, some relevant i with B_i<=K alpha_i | It is existential and hereditary over all actual reached restrictions. Requiring every coordinate is strictly unnecessary and defeated by the archived sparse split. The even weaker exact C-certificate is some i with B_i<=C alpha_i-JS_i. |
| Signed Booleanity | All signed XOR identities; L<=B<=L+bI | One finite K, every real coefficient vector obeying ALL signed constraints, some relevant i with L_i<=K alpha_i | This gives C=K+3/ln2. Aggregate L<=KI is a stronger, tensor-additive implementation target. Normalization, low-order constraints, a controlled phase marginal, and unsigned cancellation do not supply this edge-log payment. |

The signed-coordinate target is smaller than the aggregate signed target in the sense that aggregate implies selection at the same K. The signed target is not a new proof merely because Booleanity has been written algebraically. The A031 positive single-anchor Cauchy remainder is actually false as a universal O(I) target on Boolean tensors; replacing it by S/SG preserves a live target and avoids repackaging a refutation as an open lemma.

## 4. Full witness residual on a low-energy, zero-erasure family

Let g_n(x)=1-2*1_{x=(1,...,1)} on n>=2 bits and F_n(x,z)=g_n(x_1 z,...,x_n z), with d=n+1 and N=2^n. Its spectrum is p_empty=(1-2/N)^2 and p_A=4/N^2 on every nonempty even-cardinality A subset [d]; odd sets have zero mass. Consequently

    H(F_n)=H(g_n), B(F_n)=L(F_n)=0,
    I(F_n)=2d/N ->0, v=4(N-1)/N^2.

This follows by the injective parity lift of spectral labels and sum of cardinalities over even subsets. Every spectral edge has at least one zero endpoint. The n=2 case and all boundary atoms are included.

Choose the output-complement -F_n as a single arbitrary gate h, full ordered support 1,...,d, M=1, lambda=1, pi=Pr(h=1)=1/N, and a point posterior on its only partition-forest run for each A. Output complementation leaves p,H,I unchanged. The conditioned forest theorem permits an arbitrary nonconstant gate with an ordered support; regularity is required only in the later fractional pivotal-witness theorem, which is not used here. Each run has one root, one selected subset A, and zero children. Thus no attachment/label/posterior term is omitted. In particular

    Lambda+log2 Z=log2 pi=-n,
    B_lambda(A)=n+(6/5)sum_{j=1}^{|A|}log2(i_j/j),

where i_1<...<i_|A| are its ranks. The root rarity cancels EXACTLY against conditioning. Hence the complete R is

    R=(6/5)sum_{A!=empty}p_A log2[(product_j i_j)/|A|!].

Because i_j<=d-|A|+j,

    1 <= (product_j i_j)/|A|! <= binom(d,|A|) <=2^d.

Therefore R<=(6/5)vd <=(24/5)d/N=(12/5)I(F_n), with strict improvement by the factor (N-1)/N if retained. This explicitly closes J at K=12/5 on the family and gives the forest-derived H<=(2+Kref+12/5)I. It is a calibration on a known easy family, not a new universal class result or improved FEI constant. Its purpose is to show that B=0 can coexist with a nonzero, completely paid reference residual: neither statistic measures the other's missing cost pointwise.

The same one-gate construction for an arbitrary Boolean source only yields the rank upper bound (6/5)E log2 binom(d,|S|), which can lose log(d/I). The unjustified universal step is paying this rank expectation or choosing a better representation/reference for EVERY seed; the family computation does not do that. For general parity lifts a B=0 leaf still contains the arbitrary seed.
