The primary coding source is source_material/reductions/paper/forests.tex: eq:forestpacking, eq:forestrootconstants, eq:forestconditioning, eq:forestcost, thm:posteriorforest, and the full queue definition. In particular child labels use lambda_l, not a uniform-neighbor distribution. Conditioning divides successful-run mass by Z even when failure/nontermination has positive probability.

A006's 20261011T210855Z-fleet05-A006-posterior-forest-optimization/REPORT.md was read, especially its sunflower law, all-partition enumeration, normalizer, and proposed shared-r-core extension. ## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### 1. The exact conditioned initial-root law

For x_l=lambda_l pi_l and a=sum x_l>0, set D=product_l(1+x_l)-1. Then Z=D/(D+1) and Lambda+log Z=log D. Every nonempty initial root set R has probability product_{l in R}x_l/D after conditioning. This is an actual normalized root-set law: expansion of the product sums its numerators to D. Successful-run probabilities subsequently multiply this root probability by all remaining draw probabilities; no termination assumption is introduced.

Expansion gives D>=a. Also D<=exp(a)-1<=a exp(a), the last inequality following from integral_0^a exp(t)dt<=a exp(a). Thus

    log D = log a + delta, 0<=delta<=a log e.

One conditioned root cancels one common rarity scale, rather than every rarity occurrence. If x_l=t c_l with fixed c_l>0 and root count k, then

    log D + sum_{l in R} log(1/x_l)
      = (k-1)log(1/t) + log(sum c_l)
          + sum_{l in R}log(1/c_l) + delta.

So a fixed k>=2 retains a divergent (k-1)log(1/t) cost as t decreases to zero. This is noncancellation for that root pattern, not a counterexample to a full variational code or FEI.

For singleton roots restricted to an eligible set E, averaging root-label-plus-rarity cost and subtracting root-label entropy has the exact optimum

    inf_eta [log D + E_eta log(1/x_l)-H(eta)]
       = log[D/sum_{l in E}x_l]
       = log[a/sum_{l in E}x_l]+delta,
    eta_l=x_l/sum_{l in E}x_l.

This follows by subtracting the displayed value and obtaining KL(eta||eta_*). It only optimizes the root portion, not root-dependent subsequent draws. Distinct initial roots give distinct actual runs; no count of abstract forest descriptions supplies this entropy.

### 2. A one-root construction whenever the spectrum meets the shared core

Write a spectral set as U union T, U in C and T in the data coordinates. When U is nonempty, choose a gate l0, assign all of U to it, and assign every data coordinate in T to a gate containing it. Only gates owning a coordinate are visited. Gate l0 has an edge to every other visited gate through any fixed u in U, because all gates contain C. Prescribe a star rooted at l0 with these attachments and a fixed child order. The directed incidence graph has a unique source strongly connected component containing l0: l0 reaches every vertex; any component with an edge into it would be mutually reachable. Thus this is a permitted partition forest with exactly one root and one visit per used gate.

When U is empty, use any assignment of T to relevant gates and any source-component spanning forest. Every used gate owns at least one distinct coordinate, so 1<=K<=|T|. These are finite successful queue runs with positive probability: all sizes, subsets, child counts, attachment multisets and positive child labels are prescribed. A deterministic choice suffices; any posterior supported on these runs has the same root-count bounds. The fixed generator and lambda are independent of U,T.

If U is nonempty and T empty, choose a single ordered cover per gate, with the r core coordinates first in the same order. For each l, the run selecting subset U at root l with no children has B_l=log(1/lambda_l)+log(1/(p rho_l)) plus the same rank term. Averaging with eta_l=lambda_l rho_l/s subtracts H(eta) once. Therefore the normalized label-plus-rarity contribution is exactly delta, and the remaining common rank term is retained. Root-dependent subset normalizers and other actual run choices belong to the complete cost, not to this exact B-component identity.

### 3. The Boolean spectral suppression of exceptional multiple roots

Since A_r has coefficient p at every core subset, for (U,T) different from (empty,empty),

    Fhat(U union T)=2p Bhat(T), Fhat(empty)=2pb-1.

Parseval gives v=4pb(1-pb). Direct physical edge probabilities give

    I(F)=p I(g)+2rp b=I_data+I_core,
    I(g)=4 sum_T |T| Bhat(T)^2.

The entire nonempty-data, empty-core spectral mass is 4p^2 b(1-b). Every other nonempty spectral set has exactly one root in the construction above. With E denoting the unconditioned spectral-weighted sum over nonempty sets and their selected forest laws,

    E K-v=sum_{T nonempty}4p^2 Bhat(T)^2(E[K|empty,T]-1)
       <=4p^2 sum_T |T| Bhat(T)^2=p^2 I(g).

It is nonnegative. By elementary induction r 2^(-r)<=1/2 for r>=1, so r(E K-v)<=I_data/2. This all-r estimate is an analytic finite-to-infinite bridge, not inferred from a finite search.

### 4. Cancellation and payment

Here a=ps, s=sum lambda_l rho_l<=1, and log(1/pi_l)=r+log(1/rho_l). Substitution into the exact root normalization yields

    T_root=v(-r+log s+delta)+r E K+E base rarity.

This proves the identity in the target. Further,

    v delta<=4pb *p s log e
       <=(2ps/r)(log e) I_core <=(log e) I_core.

Together with the previous subsection and log s<=0, the total extra common-core contribution is at most I_data/2+(log e)I_core<=(log e)I(F). No negative normalization was removed before locating its cancellation. This theorem allows arbitrary overlapping data supports and arbitrary Boolean g, but leaves their complexity in the base rarity and other forest terms. It therefore supplies no argument for unrestricted FEI by itself.

### 5. Exact heterogeneous-petal excess

Take disjoint private conjunctions of widths w_l>=1. Put rho_l=2^(-w_l), Q=product(1-rho_l), b=1-Q. Assign private spectral coordinates to their own petal. When U empty there are no incidence edges, hence every touched petal must be a root and K is its touched-block count. When U nonempty use the star above.

Write B=1-product_l(1-b_l). Each complementary factor has squared Fourier norm 1-rho_l; its nonempty spectral mass is rho_l(1-rho_l). By disjoint-support tensor factorization,

    sum_{T nonempty} Bhat(T)^2=b(1-b)=Qb,
    sum_T K(T)Bhat(T)^2=Q sum_l rho_l.

Hence E K-v=4p^2 Q(sum_l rho_l-b), exactly. Nonnegativity also follows from the event union bound b<=sum rho_l. For m>=2 the difference is positive. Exact cancellation fails on some sets, yet its expectation is O(I), uniformly in r,m and all widths. These are genuine overlapping gates with read m, not duplicated representations.

### 6. A fixed paid scale prior, without a free adaptive scale

For j>=0 take lambda_l^(j)=2^(-j)lambda_l and w_j=1/[(j+1)(j+2)]. The weights telescope to one and each entire conditioned reference Q_{*,j} is a subprobability, with its own s_j, Lambda_j, Z_j and every subsequent queue choice. Q_mix=sum_j w_j Q_{*,j} is therefore a subprobability. Choosing component j=0 globally pays log(1/w_0)=1 on every nonempty spectral set, i.e. exactly v. Since v<=4pb<=2 I_core/r<=2 I(F), the normalization/core component plus this paid prior is bounded by E base rarity+(2+log e)I(F).

This is only a component bound inside the full reference inequality. Its unbounded base costs are not dropped or claimed paid. If one chooses a different j, log D_j and its full B_j must stay together and the selection penalty must be paid; -v log s_j is not a free gain independent of the positive lambda_j label costs. No output-dependent scale is selected in the displayed estimate.

### 7. First unsupported line for a whole-cost theorem

No gap is claimed in the stated root-component proof, but independent reconstruction is pending. The first unsupported line for G-J-COST is that the remaining expectation of gate labels, base rarity, ranks and subset congestion minus the remaining actual-run entropy has an absolute influence bound for this general activation representation. The theorem does not establish that line. In particular arbitrary g remains embedded here, consistent with the source's rare-activation hardness reductions.

# A007 stage 2: reviewed incidence theorem and full activated-tribes ledger

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

All logarithms and entropy are in bits except natural logarithms explicitly used in the convex dual. Primary packet A007, bridge C020 / lead target PI-J. First, independently review the whole A009 INCIDENCE_OPTIMIZATION.tex artifact. Second, fulfill the lead's requested activation closure, retaining every conditioned posterior residual term.

For all integers r,w>=1 let m=2^w, let C be r fresh core coordinates, and let m disjoint private blocks each contain w coordinates. Let A_r be the positive core conjunction and B the OR of positive private conjunctions b_l. Define F=2 A_r B-1. Its prime implicants are exactly the m conjunctions A_r b_l. Use one ordered cover per gate, with common core first, then private coordinates, and fixed lambda_l=1/m. The weights satisfy all incidence constraints; the representation/generator are fixed across output sets. Put p=2^-r, rho=2^-w=1/m, Q=(1-rho)^m, b=1-Q, gamma=6/5, v=Var(F), I_c=2rpb and I_d=2p Q m w rho/(1-rho).

There exist actual partition-forest posterior laws with the complete source residual

    R = v(Lambda+log Z) + E[E_eta B_lambda-H(eta)] <= (26/5) I(F).

Here B_lambda includes labels once per used gate, every root rarity, all subset congestion and rank costs, and M_l=1 is explicit. Expectations on nonempty spectral sets use their unconditioned masses. A normalized prior on complete scaled references, chosen globally at j=0, adds exactly v<=2I, so the residual plus prior is <=(36/5)I. This is a PI-J coding closure, not a new FEI class or an all-function theorem. Size/child-count/multiset constants are retained through K_ref in the underlying full entropy bound H(F)<=h2(v)+K_ref I+R.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### A. Whole A009 review

The independent reconstruction is saved immediately in REVIEW_A009.md. Verdict HOLDS at stated scopes, with no repair supplied: fixed-law log-weight convex program and sufficient exact dual certificates; actual duplicate-dictator counterexample to joint posterior convexity; full r,m singleton-petal R<=8I continuation and paid prior; geometric sunflower pressure <41I; and affine-plane positive pressure >=7I log((Q+1)/2), all prime powers Q>=16, for every feasible vector and pivotal tie decomposition.

The affine-plane result only bounds positive coordinatewise pressure. Its full conditioned residual, PI-J and FEI are not refuted. Independent finite checks reconstruct physical boundaries at Q=2,3, line geometry at six prime Q, all four rational KKT certificates, and the singleton activation ledger, without importing A009's code. The analytic review proof covers every Q>=16, including nonprime prime powers.

### A2. New retained-normalizer affine-plane consequence

The reviewed affine-plane argument can be strengthened for the coordinatewise fractional bound itself, keeping its negative normalization rather than hoping it is negligible. For every prime power q>=16, all feasible positive weights, and every pivotal tie decomposition, put rho=2^-q, k=q+1, N=qk, u=sum_l lambda_l and z_j=sum_{l through j}lambda_l. Then

    P + v log Z >= [7 log((q+1)/2)-5] I.

This is still not a lower bound on the full partition-forest residual or PI-J. It proves the coordinatewise fractional bound cannot obtain an absolute constant even by optimizing all weights and retaining its negative v log Z.

To prove it, retain the row mass in Holder instead of replacing it by one. The reviewed cap q_{j,l}<=2/k gives

    P >=7I log(k/2)-(11/5) sum_j alpha_j log z_j
       >=7I log(k/2)+(11/5)I log(q/u).

The second step uses point transitivity of the affine-plane Boolean function, hence constant alpha_j, and Jensen: average_j z_j=u/q, because each line contains q points. Incidence gives 0<u<=q. Root product expansion gives D>=rho u, so Z=D/(1+D)>=rho u/(1+rho u), and therefore

    log Z >=-q+log u-log(1+rho q).

This inequality retains the entire negative normalizer. The reviewed clean-event influence bound and the union bound on the true probability give

    I>=2qN rho(1-epsilon_q), v<=4N rho,
    qv/I<=2/(1-epsilon_q)<=4, v/I<=4/q<=1/4.

Thus v-(11/5)I<0. Adding the two displayed bounds, the scale-dependent part is (v-(11/5)I)log u, minimized at the largest allowed u=q. Consequently

    P+v log Z >=7I log(k/2)+v log q-qv-v log(1+rho q)
       >=7I log(k/2)-5I,

since log q>=0, rho q<=1, qv<=4I and v<=I. This is an all-weight proof, not a statement about uniform weights only. In particular making root weights arbitrarily small makes the label pressure increase faster than the negative normalizer can refund it. A009's original scope remains accepted; this stronger retained-normalizer consequence is A007's new contribution and awaits independent review.

### B. Exact Boolean law and real forest laws

For a private Fourier set T, write T_l=T intersect block l, s_l=|T_l|, t=#nonempty T_l. Product expansion of B=1-product_l(1-b_l) gives Bhat(empty)=b and, for T nonempty,

    Bhat(T)=-product_l [ -rho if T_l nonempty; 1-rho otherwise ].

For every nonempty U union T, U in C, Fhat(U,T)=2p Bhat(T), and Fhat(empty)=2pb-1. Hence

    v=4pb(1-pb), I(F)=I_c+I_d,
    E t =4p Q m rho =4p Q,
    E K-v=4p^2 Q(m rho-b)=4p^2 Q^2.

The touched-block formulas follow by tensor norms: each complementary factor has total squared norm 1-rho and nonempty mass rho(1-rho); summing contributions of a touched block gives Q rho. Physical edge counts give I_c=2rpb and I_d=2p Q m w rho/(1-rho), independently of spectral calculation.

The posterior consists only of these distinct finite queue runs:

- U nonempty, T empty: one gate owns all U; choose the m roots uniformly, with no children. H(eta)=log m.
- U and T nonempty: choose a touched petal as root, assign it all U and its T_l, and give every other touched petal its own T_l. Attach each child through the first selected core coordinate; choose all ordered child-gate sequences. There are t*(t-1)!=t! distinct events, chosen uniformly, so H(eta)=log(t!).
- U empty, T nonempty: each touched block owns its coordinates and is an isolated source component. All t touched gates are roots, queued in the fixed initial order; H(eta)=0.

All chosen subsets are nonempty, partition the output and lie in the owning support. Each gate is visited once. For mixed sets the root reaches every used gate through the selected core coordinate; its strongly connected component is the unique source. Thus these are actual admissible partition forests. Child permutations are distinct chronological random draws, not alternative descriptions of a single run. The small-domain checker constructs their actual ownership/child traces independently and checks uniqueness.

### C. Root rarity and normalization, kept together

All pi_l=p rho, so every root rarity is r+w. With fixed lambda_l=1/m,

    D=product_l(1+lambda_l pi_l)-1=(1+p rho/m)^m-1,
    Lambda+log Z=log D=-(r+w)+delta,
    0<=delta<=p rho log e.

The last bound follows from p rho<=D<=p rho exp(p rho), not from floating point. Therefore the exact root part, including normalization, is

    v log D+(r+w) E K = v delta+(r+w)(E K-v).

Core rarity and all petal rarity are canceled once per nonempty set; exceptional extra roots retain both scales. Since E K-v<=4p^2 Q m rho,

    (r+w)(E K-v)/I_d <=2p(1-rho)(1+r/w)<=2,

using p<=1/2 and rp<=1/2. Also

    v delta<=4pb *p rho log e<=(log e)I_c.

This pays the entire root/normalization contribution by 2I_d+(log e)I_c. No base rarity is left unpaid in this uniform-width closure. The negative normalization has already done its mathematical job before taking an upper bound.

### D. Every rank term

F_l^j is the logarithm of the physical position in gate l divided by the selected-predecessor count plus one. For a selected core coordinate of rank j, the preceding core selection is Bin(j-1,1/2), including on core-only spectra. For a selected private coordinate at internal rank j, the preceding core selection and preceding private selection in its own block are jointly Bin(r+j-1,1/2). Indeed, conditioning on this private coordinate being selected makes the other private coordinates in that block uniform: the conjunction's nonempty Fourier coefficients all have the same magnitude. The coefficient 2p Bhat(T) is independent of U, so the core subset is independently uniform.

This conditional assertion remains valid under our root posterior: every private coordinate is assigned to its own gate; its F_l^j depends on A, not on which touched gate became root. Core coordinates see the same order at every root.

For any rank h, the identity E[1/(1+Bin(h-1,1/2))]=2(1-2^-h)/h and Jensen give

    E log[h/(1+Bin(h-1,1/2))] <=1.

Consequently the whole natural rank term (gamma/ln2) E sum_j F_tau(j)^j is at most gamma I(F). Cover multiplicity is exactly zero because M_l=1, not because it has been discarded.

### E. Every subset-congestion and gate-label term

The root of a mixed star owns U union T_l and has s_root=kappa_root, hence zero congestion. A private leaf owns s_l>=1 coordinates while kappa_l=|U|+s_l. Its congestion is gamma s_l log(1+|U|/s_l). Core-only and empty-core sets have zero congestion. Pointwise s log(1+u/s)<=u log e, so the full congestion is at most gamma |U| t log e on nonempty-data sets. Conditional on T nonempty, U is uniform, yielding

    E congestion <= gamma (r/2) E t log e
       =2gamma rp Q m rho log e <=gamma(log e)I_c.

For the last inequality Q m rho<=1-Q=b: expand (1-rho)^-m>=(1+rho)^m>=1+m rho and rearrange. This is the key activation payment: a shared-core subset charge is paid once from the core influence, without charging log(r+1) separately to every private spectral coordinate.

Each used gate contributes L_l=log m=w. On core-only spectra, posterior entropy log m cancels that label exactly. On every other spectral set there are t used gates, with nonnegative posterior entropy, hence

    E labels-H(eta) <=w E t=4p Q m rho w
       =2(1-rho)I_d<=2I_d.

The exact ledger retains the mixed-set saving -log(t!) rather than requiring it for the upper bound. Every label is counted once per used gate, not once per selected coordinate.

### F. Full residual assembly and paid prior

Summing C, D and E retains the whole B cost and its actual-run entropy:

    R <=(4+gamma)I_d + [gamma+(1+gamma)log e]I_c.

Since gamma=6/5 and ln2>2/3 by the positive atanh series, log e<3/2. Thus the second coefficient is <9/2 and the first is 26/5. This proves R<=(26/5)I for all r,w without increasing a computational dimension.

For a fixed prior use lambda_l^(j)=2^-j/m, w_j=1/[(j+1)(j+2)], j>=0, on entire conditioned generators. The references are subprobabilities and the weights sum to one. Choosing component j=0 globally adds v log(1/w_0)=v<=4pb<=2I_c/r<=2I. Thus R+paid prior<=(36/5)I. Each component keeps its own D_j, labels and all costs together. No free output-dependent scale choice is used.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

The complete analytic proof is LIFT.md. For each base actual forest and selected nonempty core U, choose an old root to own U; preserve every data assignment, edge attachment and within-coordinate child sequence, and connect all other former roots through selected core coordinates. Deleting core-attached edges and replaying the old fixed queue order recovers the unique base event. Therefore the construction counts distinct actual generator events. Its added multiplicity is (c-1)! binom(|U|+c-2,c-1), rather than |U|^(c-1).

The selected mass is p S(T)G_U(T). For empty selected core this equals the full partition-forest mass by a bijection; for nonempty selected core it is a lower bound on full mass. For core-only sets, summing one-gate outcomes gives p beta a 2^(-C_U). Keeping log D'=Lambda'+log Z' yields exact normalizer log[D'/(pD)] and the signed bound <=log beta only after the identity is tested. The core-only rare-root/name block cancels to delta' in [0,p beta a log e].

The quantitative refinement bounds private rank shift pointwise by gamma |T|log(r/|U|), then averages over the binomial selected core. The exact reciprocal-binomial identity removes any log r loss, paying all new private ranks by 3p I(g). Extra empty-core roots cost <=p I(g)/2. Core ranks across both data and core-only sectors total <=(6/5)I_core(F); core-only normalization costs <=(log e/4)I_core(F). The remaining signed Psi is defined with the SAME base posterior, preserving congestion and scale labels rather than optimizing those pieces separately.

The first unsupported line is Psi<=K I(F), with a dimension-independent K, for suitable globally fixed feasible weights on arbitrary overlapping signed prime tables. A paid base R_g alone does not supply it. It is not part of the claimed theorem. REVIEW_A006.md independently reconstructs the whole determinant-moment claim, including actual chronology, rational bounds, SCC factorization boundary and positive-mass sharpness, with no repair. Its local acceptance does not imply an all-dimensional influence bound.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

POSTERIOR_TAIL.md proves the all-s,N tail. Write A(v)=prod(v_i/s)^v_i=2^(-sH). A multinomial probability implies [s!/prod v_i!]A(v)<=1. The original finite-N labels satisfy binom(N,k)N^-k<=1/k!, while the k=1 weight is exactly one, giving posterior normalizer >=1. Each record is therefore bounded by (s)_(k-1)/k!*2^(-sH/5). Sum positive ordered compositions BEFORE paying the entropy loss to get C_s.

With n=s-1, C_s<=sum_j binom(n,j)binom(n+j,j)=sum_j binom(n,j)^2 2^j. Vandermonde proves the identity directly. Nonnegative cross terms bound it by (1+sqrt(2))^(2n)<6^n for n>0. Integrating the resulting exponential tail proves E H<=5log2(6). The strict rational constant sign is 6^5=7776<8192=2^13. Neither an asymptotic estimate nor floating-point entropy is needed.

REVIEW_A015.md independently reconstructs its entire fixed-assignment envelope, sharp coefficient 1/5 and explicit error, actual parity prime realization, same-posterior Jensen step, signed controller ledger and exact owned-size law. No repair is supplied. Its proposed tail was explicitly unsupported in the source; our proof closes it downstream. The source diagnostic is Markdown, not a fabricated registered upstream claim. Separately, A015's independent acceptance record of A007-DISTRIBUTED-CORE-COMPONENT-LIFT v1 is copied at unchanged statement/hash.

In the full-support regime beta=1,c=1,L_0=1. Retain the exact signed identity

    Psi_dist=p Var(g)log[D'/(pD)]-p sum_T P_g(T)E_U log J_dist,U(T).

The reviewed envelope and tail give an upper bound p Var(g)log[D'/(pD)]+(pr/2)Var(g)log2(6). Only after writing this is D'<=pD used. Thus Psi_dist<=(1-b)log2(6)I_core(F), and

    R_F<=pR_g+(7/2)pI(g)
             +[6/5+log2(e)/4+(1-b)log2(6)]I_core(F).

The complete inherited reference cost pR_g, its physical rank/location information, h2(Var F) and K_ref I(F) are retained. They are not replaced by H(g), assumed zero, or charged twice. Surrogate posterior mass is not actual queue probability.

GENERAL_BUDGET.md advances the arbitrary-support continuation. With a_l=s_l/s and s_l<=kappa_l<=s, define K_tau(u)=sum_l a_l[s log(s/kappa_l)-(s+u)log((s+u)/(kappa_l+u))]>=0. Unequal root rarities give the valid retained credit sum_root log(1/pi)-max_root log(1/pi), because the exact rooted component counts sum to (u)_(c-1). Jensen on the SAME old posterior gives one signed expected budget containing beta labels, K_tau, ownership entropy, rare-root credit, factorial event credit and log[D'/(pD)]. No term is silently dropped.

# A008 gate-label compression: dictionary-first code

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

For every finite fixed gate table with positive rational structural probabilities and supports covering the relevant coordinates, construct a finite prefix code describing each used gate name once and decoding the complete spectral set. Give the full ledger, including dictionary selection, local subsets, roots, attachments, codebook selection, output ambiguity and rounding. All entropy and ideal costs are in bits; Boolean influence is E|S| on the uniform sign cube. WHOLE_SET_CODEC.tex contains the complete proof and precise quantifiers.

One combined candidate is supplied: the general codec, its explicitly distinguished record/output versions, and a quantified dictionary-reference application on f_m=x0-(1+x0)prod_i(1-x_i)/2 for all integers m>=1. Its normalized nonempty output-reference expectation is <(25/4)I(f_m), and the corresponding full-output prefix length is <(33/4)I(f_m). This is a new reference-cost theorem on A006's existing sunflower benchmark, not a new Boolean FEI class. Full FEI remains OPEN. Independent acceptance is pending.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

The complete execution protocol fei-execution-protocol/2, revision 2026-10-11-execution-v2, governs this run. README, CURRENT_STATUS, the claim/obstruction/packet indices, STREAM_NEXT_ACTIONS and the campaign plan were consulted, together with the A008 contract in AGENT_ASSIGNMENTS. BOARD.md was read at stage start (snapshot 2026-10-11 22:05 UTC). B02/STREAM_NOTES.md was absent at the exact supplied path; no direction was inferred. There was no previous A008 mathematical run.

The supplement CAMPAIGN, CLAIMS, COUNTEREXAMPLES, SEED_LEMMAS and NATIVE_MAPPING were consulted, as were the curated source-note scopes. Relevant interfaces are C008/C020 (complete reference cost), X012 (addressing), and SEED_LEMMAS section 2 (paid mixture). No historical erasure, phase, biased-threshold or coherent-product assertion is used as a proof premise.

Context threshold_paper_38pp.txt sections 5.2, 5.3, 5.6, Proposition 5.5 and 5.7 were read for the existing queue and whole-witness scope. The earlier threshold version was inspected for context. Percolation versions were inspected for their introduction and section 2 refinement-code scope; their spatial hypotheses are not imported. Text files use cp1252; hashes bind original bytes rather than recoded copies.

A006's complete report, runs/20261011T210855Z-fleet05-A006-posterior-forest-optimization/REPORT.md, was read. It supplies the nonduplication comparison: its queue already visits each used gate once; posterior entropy is indispensable on its common singleton. Its bounds are R<=43I/10 and complete queue expectation <=23I/2, or <=25I/2 with its selected scale prior. These are contextual comparison targets; this proof derives the Boolean law and the new reference independently and does not import A006 as an accepted theorem. The supplied reports/runs index was inspected; no earlier B02 report exists there. out/SOURCES.json contains byte hashes and specific locators of proof-relevant files.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

The dictionary generator first chooses k, then a set F of k gate names, then one nonempty local subset per named gate. With E_k the elementary symmetric polynomial in the fixed name weights eta, its normalized probability is

    a(r)=kappa_k * prod_{l in F}(eta_l)/E_k(eta) * prod_{l in F}q_l(B_l).

The names are one set in increasing order, not one label per coordinate or per occurrence. The exact ideal cost is -log kappa_k+log E_k-sum log eta_l-sum log q_l(B_l). Finite rational probabilities give computable ceil(-log probability) lengths and a canonical prefix trie, with one total rounding overhead less than one. Normalization follows by summing successive normalized finite choices. All codebooks derive from fixed structural data, without knowledge of the sampled spectral set or a free spectrum-dependent selector.

The decoder unions the subsets and assigns each coordinate to the least containing selected gate. It discards empty owners, builds the directed incidence graph, chooses the least vertex in each source strongly connected component, and builds a breadth-first forest with least available attachment coordinates. Thus roots and attachments are deterministically reconstructed after full decoding. Their conditional cost is zero in this generator. This does NOT remove the root normalizer from the original queue: it replaces the queue by a different finite normalized experiment. CODE_COST_LEDGER.json records every term and this distinction.

For Q(A)=sum_{r:D(r)=A}a(r), the output reference is normalized. Finite Gibbs gives H(P)<=h2(v)+sum_{A!=empty}P(A)(-log Q(A)). A finite output prefix trie also decodes A directly, and may compute its lexicographically first record. It does not decode the random latent record. The identity inf_nu[E_nu c-H(nu)]=-log Q(A) is proved by the explicit finite KL calculation. Consequently posterior entropy is legitimate only as quotient/reference credit; it cannot be subtracted from transmitted witness bits. This is the exact final-output ambiguity accounting.

The addressing table uses one uniformly chosen address gate and one uniform control subset; its decoder receives exactly 2a bits, with H=2a and I=1+a/2. The gate name occurs once. No children, roots requiring choices, subset sizes requiring additional bits, or ambiguity remain in that dedicated table. Choosing it at half-weight in a larger mixture would add one paid selector bit; it is not silently free.

For the sunflower choose eta_i=1, kappa_k=(m+1)/(m*k*(k+1)), and q_i uniform on {0},{i},{0,i}. These size weights sum exactly to one. The complete output formulas in WHOLE_SET_CODEC.tex sum every actual dictionary trace, including those with extra common-only gates. In particular Q({0})=sum_k kappa_k*3^(-k)>=1/6. For every private nonempty T of size t, Q(T)=kappa_t/[binom(m,t)*3^t], and Q({0} union T) is at least this value. Their costs are <=3m+2t.

The exact Boolean law is P(empty)=4^(-m), P({0})=(1-2^(-m))^2, and P(T)=P({0} union T)=4^(-m) for T nonempty; I=1+(m-1)2^(-m)>=1. The total rare-set upper bound is r_m=8m*2^(-m)-6m*4^(-m). It is 5/2 at m=1 and 13/4 at m=2, and decreases thereafter because 4^(m+1)(r_m-r_(m+1))=16(m-1)2^m-18m+6>=46m-58>0. The common singleton costs <3 bits. Thus the full nonempty reference expectation is <25/4<=25I/4. Empty/nonempty entropy and prefix rounding each add at most I, giving expected physical OUTPUT code length <33I/4.

There is a necessary limitation. A raw dictionary record producing {0} has cost >=log m: for k<m, binom(m,k)>=m, and for k=m, 3^m>=m. Its probability is <=1/m. So insisting on revealing a latent witness causes an expected cost >=(1-2^(-m))^2 log m. The bounded result is for the output quotient, not a raw name-transmitting record. This exact distinction prevents a false universal gate-compression claim.

The first unsupported universal line is the existence of a globally fixed structural dictionary table, or a paid normalized mixture, whose ENTIRE output-reference expectation is O(I) for every Boolean f. Local subset costs and dictionary size choices are unpaid in general. The dictionary codec proves decodability and a restricted cost payment, not that line. It is not a bound on the original B_lambda residual and imposes no fractional-incidence condition because it uses a different normalized reference.

# A008 stage 2: reviewed sunflower forests and unequal block-forest payment

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

The B02 lead assigns A008 to reconstruct A006 v1 as a whole, then close the positive block-forest side for every read-once DNF. Both tasks are complete locally. The new theorem covers every m>=1 and every positive integer width tuple (w_1,...,w_m), arbitrary literal signs, disjoint supports, and one fixed prime-implicant table and feasible incidence vector lambda_l=1. Entropy and ideal costs are in bits; the source F statistic is in natural logs and its conversion is explicit. READ_ONCE_DNF.tex gives the complete proof.

The ORIGINAL conditioned queue has residual

    R <= (16/5+log2 e) I < 47I/10.

The actual finite once-per-block run probabilities, rather than the coarser generic forest bound, have complete nonempty cost

    C_pf <= (26/5+2log2 3+2log2 e) I.

Including the empty/nonempty entropy split gives h2(v)+C_pf<67I/5. This is a PI-J reference-cost closure on an already known FEI class. It is not a new Boolean FEI class or a universal bound. Optional prefix rounding and optional selector costs are not included or silently charged to I. Full FEI remains OPEN; the new result awaits independent review.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### Whole A006 review

REVIEW_A006.md records HOLDS at the original stated scope, with no repair supplied. Independent construction starts with every assignment of spectral coordinates to eligible gates, computes source SCCs, enumerates their root choices and incoming parent maps, checks reachability, enumerates attachments and chronological child-label orders, then deduplicates actual queue keys. It does not import or execute the author's checker or assume the star classification before enumerating.

The all-m proof then reconstructs why private-only sets have one isolated-root run, common singleton has m runs, and common+private sets have t! inside-root runs and (m-t)t! outside-root runs. Formal residual weights are independently summed as a-polynomials, a=2^-6/5. The complete actual probabilities multiply root conditioning, subset sizes, elementary normalizers, zero-child counts, root child counts, attachment multisets and each lambda-child draw. This yields exactly A006's residual S_B and complete W formulas. The source root normalizer D lies in [1/4,1/3); the two binomial recurrences prove R<=43I/10 and actual nonempty cost<=23I/2 for all m. The deterministic residual diverges on the common singleton. The normalized scale prior adds exactly v bits at component zero, preserving A006's 53I/10 and 25I/2 conclusions. Complete entropy inference still includes h2(v). No unsupported line remains in the reviewed restricted statement.

### Fixed unequal-block prime table and exact decoder

For disjoint clauses, p_l=2^-w_l, q_l=1-p_l, Q0=product q_l, v=4Q0(1-Q0), and lambda_l=1. The clauses are precisely the prime implicants: a partial assignment containing no full clause can be extended to make one unspecified literal false in every block. Signed literals can be flipped physically without changing the squared spectrum or influence.

Every spectral coordinate has a unique owner. Each used block is an isolated source SCC and hence one root; there are no children, no attachments and no extra child-order choices. Every root's selected subset is A intersect its block, so kappa=s and M=1. This is a finite terminating decoder: encode the root set once under the fixed initial-root law, read one local subset per root in fixed order, and take their union. The selected run family has no final output ambiguity and receives no posterior entropy credit. Additional successful queue runs are omitted, not presumed impossible.

The direct expansion f=1-2product_l(1-a_l) gives nonempty spectrum P(A)=4product_used p_l^2 product_unused q_l^2 and empty atom (1-2Q0)^2. Summing the other blocks yields mu_l=P(A hits block l)=4p_lQ0. Conditional on a block hit, its subset is uniform on all nonempty subsets; conditional on a coordinate being selected, all other within-block selections are independent fair bits. Physical pivotal events independently give I_l=2Q0 w_l p_l/q_l and I=sum I_l.

### Original residual paid in full

Put D=product_l(1+p_l)-1, so Lambda+log Z=log D exactly. The entire residual is

    R=v log D+sum_l mu_l w_l+(6/5) E sum_selected log(rank/(selected_predecessors+1)).

Labels L_l=0 because lambda=1, but the initial root-name selection cost is paid, not free: its exact contribution is v log D+sum mu_l w_l. Root rarity is <=2I. For normalization, T=sum w_l p_l/q_l>=2sum p_l, since w_l/q_l>=2. Also log D<=sum log(1+p_l)<=log(e)sum p_l. Thus v log D<=log(e)I, retaining its sign. This has a direct block proof and agrees with the baseline weighted activation estimate.

Conditional on selecting rank r, predecessor count X is Bin(r-1,1/2). The exact reciprocal identity E[r/(1+X)]=2(1-2^-r)<=2 and log concavity give expected rank cost <=1 bit per selected coordinate. Therefore the full rank bill is <=(6/5)I. Adding all components gives R<47I/10. This replaces the earlier loose factorial/concavity checkpoint R<13I/2; it is the same mechanism with the sharper exact conditional law.

### Actual queue cost, rather than generic K_ref overhead

With theta_s(j)=min(1,(s/rank(j))^(6/5)) and t_l(B)=[1/(s(s+1))]product_B theta_s/Z_s, the one specified successful run has

    q_pf(A)=D^-1 product_used [(p_l/2)*t_l(A_l)].

Every factor is retained: roots and their absence, subset sizes, subset normalizers/weights, and probability 1/2 of child count zero. Its empty attachment multiset has probability one. Each q_pf is an actual finite conditioned event and these events are disjoint, so sum q_pf<=1. The roots are paid once per used gate. This is the ORIGINAL generator, unlike A008 v1's separate dictionary generator.

The expected root block is <=(2+log e)I. The zero-child bill is <=I. Size costs <=log3 I follow from s(s+1)<=3^s. Subset normalizers <=log(6e)I follow from sum theta_s<=6s and the elementary symmetric polynomial bound. Pointwise log^+(rank/s)<=log(rank/(selected_predecessors+1)), so the actual subset weights cost <=(6/5)I by the same conditional rank law. The resulting nonempty constant is 26/5+2log3+2log e. Gibbs with reference masses 1-v and v*q_pf(A), and h2(v)<=2I, proves full reference cost <67I/5. Rational comparisons 3^5<2^8 and ln2>2/3 certify the displayed constant without floating-point signs.

### Honest boundary

No line in the new restricted theorem remains unsupported; independent review is pending. The first unsupported universal PI-J line is a bound on the ENTIRE joint label/rank/subset expectation under one fixed feasible prime-table reference when supports overlap. The exact uniform local law and forced ownership proved here do not follow from Booleanity alone. Neither A009's coordinatewise pressure obstruction nor A007's root cancellation settles that joint term. Physical prefix rounding adds less than one whole-message bit and is not claimed to be O(I) when I tends to zero.

SOURCE ~\brain_workers\FEI100_20261011\agents\A009\runs\20261011T223308Z-fleet05-A009-fractional-incidence-optimization\REPORT.md
# A009 fractional incidence optimization

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

This packet certifies one global incidence vector for a complete fixed-posterior forest residual, and derives its convex optimization dual. It supplies continuation/verification evidence for the existing A006/A007 mechanism, not a third broad B02 candidate. Independent acceptance is pending; full FEI remains OPEN.

For every finite nonconstant gate union with nonempty relevant supports R_l and pi_l>0, fix probability laws eta_A on distinct actual finite partition-forest runs. All logarithms in the residual are base two; natural units are used only to formulate the convex program. Put v=sum_{A nonempty} P_f(A), c_l=sum_A P_f(A) Pr_eta_A(l visited), and let d collect every lambda-independent cost and minus the actual-run posterior entropy. Remove gates with c_l=0 if desired: no selected run uses them, so their removal preserves these runs and decreases the normalizer. For the retained gates c_l>0. With D(lambda)=product_l(1+pi_l lambda_l)-1, the exact residual for these fixed laws is

    R(lambda)=d+[v ln D(lambda)-sum_l c_l ln lambda_l]/ln 2.

The requirements are lambda_l>0 and sum_{l:j in R_l}lambda_l<=1 for every physical coordinate. The generator and its weights are fixed across output sets. This does not assert that the posterior selected here is the posterior optimal for every lambda.

For all r,m>=1, take r common fair core coordinates and m private fair coordinates. Let A_r be their core conjunction, B_m the OR of the private positive literals, and f=2 A_r B_m-1. Its m distinct gates are core conjunction AND private_l. A single ordered cover lists all core coordinates first, then that gate's private coordinate; M_l=1. The certificate lambda_l=1/m satisfies every incidence constraint, including common-coordinate sum 1 and private-coordinate sum 1/m. The complete residual of the explicit actual-run laws below satisfies R<=8 I(f), and R plus a paid complete-reference scale prior is <=10 I(f), uniformly in both r and m. This is beyond bounded-read *representations*: maximum read is m. The Boolean functions themselves are already read-once formulas and weighted thresholds; no new Boolean FEI class or novelty is claimed.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### Convexity for fixed actual-run laws

Write t_l=ln lambda_l and A_{jl}=1_{j in R_l}. Expansion gives

    D(e^t)=sum_{empty != R subset [N]} exp(sum_{l in R}(t_l+ln pi_l)).

Thus F(t)=v ln D(e^t)-c dot t is convex, being a log-sum-exp minus a linear function. Each g_j(t)=sum_l A_{jl}exp(t_l)-1 is convex. This statement does not require a convex objective in lambda coordinates. Strict feasibility holds by taking all lambda small and positive. Duplicate supports give distinct columns/draw labels and remain subject to the same coordinate rows; they must not be silently combined in a cost calculation. Every nonconstant gate has nonempty relevant support. Constant-false gates are removed; a constant-true gate makes f constant and H=I=0. Uncovered coordinates have the empty incidence sum zero. If a retained gate has c_l>0, its weight cannot be set to zero with finite fixed-posterior cost except in a simultaneous common-scaling limit where the normalizer cancels; see the boundary discussion below.

Here is a dual lower certificate without a solver. Choose a probability law nu on nonempty root sets, with marginals r_l=Pr_nu(l in R), and coordinate prices y_j>=0. Define q_l=sum_j A_{jl}y_j and b_l=c_l-v r_l. Require b_l>=0, and q_l>0 when b_l>0. With H_nat(nu)=-sum nu ln nu, define

    L(nu,y)=v[H_nat(nu)+sum_l r_l ln pi_l]-sum_j y_j
             +sum_{l:b_l>0} b_l[1+ln(q_l/b_l)].

Then inf_feasible F >= L. To prove this, the elementary finite Gibbs identity gives ln D(e^t)>=H_nat(nu)+sum_l r_l(t_l+ln pi_l). Add sum_j y_j g_j(t), which is <=0 on feasible points. Its infimum over unconstrained t separates into inf_t[-b_l t+q_l exp(t)]. For b_l,q_l>0 the value is b_l[1+ln(q_l/b_l)], attained at exp(t)=b_l/q_l. If b_l=0 the infimum is zero (also when q_l=0). If b_l<0 or b_l>0=q_l it is -infinity, hence those choices give no useful certificate. This proves weak duality directly; no unjustified interchange of supremum and infimum is used.

For a feasible positive lambda, take nu_*(R)=product_{l in R}(pi_l lambda_l)/D(lambda). Its marginals are rational for rational lambda and pi:

    r_l=pi_l lambda_l product_{k!=l}(1+pi_k lambda_k)/D.

If nonnegative rational prices satisfy

    c_l-v r_l=lambda_l q_l,
    y_j(sum_l A_{jl}lambda_l-1)=0,

the displayed dual bound equals F(ln lambda) exactly. Gibbs equality, scalar minimizer equality (including b_l=q_l=0), and complementary slackness prove the equality and global optimality. Equivalently the gradient of the convex Lagrangian vanishes, which also proves sufficiency directly. This is the claimed verifiable duality assertion: any such certificate is exact. Only the proved sufficient certificate and weak duality are asserted and used here. No necessity theorem, minimax interchange, or assertion that every instance has a finite optimizer is needed.

In particular the actual family member (r,m)=(1,2) with the fixed posterior below has v=15/16, c_l=17/32, pi_l=1/4, lambda_l=1/2, r_l=9/17. The common coordinate price y=19/272 and private prices zero give

    (15/16)(9/17)-17/32+(1/2)(19/272)=0.

This certifies a global optimum for that actual fixed-posterior instance. Three other heterogeneous examples in WEIGHT_CERTIFICATE.json certify abstract program inputs only; their c values are not asserted to be Boolean-realizable run marginals.

For the symmetric r,m family below c_l=c_total/m. Permuting t preserves feasibility and F; convex averaging therefore shows the infimum can be taken at equal weights, even if it is not attained. Set lambda_l=z/m, 0<z<=1. The scalar derivative with respect to ln z is v E_nu K-c_total, where K is root count. For m>=2 its derivative again is v Var_nu K>0, since both singleton and double root sets have positive mass. Hence there is at most one stationary scale; otherwise the upper boundary is optimal. For m>=2 our laws have c_total>v, so the objective tends to +infinity as z decreases to zero and a finite equal-weight minimizer exists. For m=1, c_total=v and the scale-dependent expression is constant. For general fixed laws, c_total=v can instead produce an unattained boundary infimum: if every output uses exactly one among several eligible gates, scaling all weights down cancels the leading ln scale. Consequently zero weights/boundary limits cannot simply be excluded when claiming the variational infimum.

### Joint posterior/weight optimization is not convex

Take the actual Boolean function f=x_1 represented by two identical dictator gates. There are two actual partition runs for its singleton spectral set, one rooted at each gate, and pi_1=pi_2=1/2. After optimizing the posterior, S_B=(lambda_1+lambda_2)/2 and the natural-unit residual is

    ln[D/S_B]=ln[1+lambda_1 lambda_2/(2(lambda_1+lambda_2))].

Put lambda_1=exp(t+s), lambda_2=exp(t-s). This becomes ln[1+exp(t)/(4 cosh s)]. At t=ln(1/4), s=0 the incidence inequality has strict slack and the second derivative in s is -1/17. Thus the full posterior-optimized objective is not convex in log weights, even for an actual Boolean example. The fixed-law convex program and its dual must not be transferred to that broader objective.

### Full shared-core residual certificate

Put p=2^-r, q=2^-m, b=1-q, gamma=6/5. For core U and private T, every nonempty set has coefficient 2p Bhat(T). Bhat(empty)=b; every nonempty private T has |Bhat(T)|=q. Therefore

    P(U union T)=4p^2 b^2 if T empty and U nonempty,
    P(U union T)=4p^2 q^2 if T nonempty (including U empty),
    v=4pb(1-pb), I_core=2rp b, I_data=2pmq.

These also follow directly from physical pivotal probabilities. Choose the following laws, fixed independently of lambda:

* If U nonempty and T empty: one gate owns U; average uniformly over all m root gates, giving H(eta)=log m.
* If U nonempty and T nonempty, t=|T|: select one of the t private gates as root, assign all U to it along with its private coordinate, and attach the other t-1 private gates through the first selected core coordinate. Average uniformly over the t root choices and (t-1)! ordered child-label sequences. There are exactly t! distinct actual queue runs, so H(eta)=log(t!).
* If U empty: each of the t private coordinates is assigned to its own gate; all t gates are roots. The source components are singleton gates, and the root queue's fixed order gives one actual run, with entropy zero.

Every run visits each used gate once, uses disjoint assigned subsets, and terminates. The root reaches every other used gate when U is nonempty, so the star is a permitted spanning forest with one source component. Ordered child draws are distinct actual random outcomes, not permutations of an abstract written edge list. The selected common attachment has positive probability under the uniform multiset draw. Every prescribed label draw is positive with lambda=1/m.

Let E denote spectral-weighted expectation on nonempty sets and these laws, K the root count, and V the used-gate count. Direct tensor summation gives

    E K-v=4p^2 q(m/2-b),
    E |T|=I_data,
    E V-v=2p q[m-2(1-q)]>0 for m>=2.

For the last identity, E V=core-only mass + E|T|, and core-only mass=4p(1-p)b^2. The difference is zero for m=1 and any r; it is positive for m>=2 because m>=2>2b.

The root rarity is r+1, and D=(1+p/(2m))^m-1. Expansion and exp(a)-1<=a exp(a), a=p/2, give

    log D=-(r+1)+delta, 0<=delta<=(p/2)log e.

Hence normalization plus rarity is exactly v delta+(r+1)(E K-v). Since K-1<=|T| on the empty-core exceptional sets,

    E K-v<=p I_data,
    (r+1)(E K-v)<=I_data,
    v delta <=(log e) I_core.

Here (r+1)2^-r<=1 and v<=4pb. Both Lambda and log Z were retained through log D; neither is silently dropped.

Write G(U)=gamma sum_{j selected in U} log(j/position_U(j)), using ranks j=1..r. Every selected core coordinate contributes this same term, regardless of root. Conditional on selection of rank j, its predecessor count is Bin(j-1,1/2). Direct binomial summation gives E[1/(1+Bin)]=2(1-2^-j)/j. Jensen for the concave log therefore gives expected log(j/(1+Bin))<=1, so the uniform-U average of G is <=gamma r/2. Also sum_T Bhat(T)^2=b (B is an indicator). Consequently

    E G(U)<=4p^2 b 2^r gamma r/2=gamma I_core.

For a private leaf the subset congestion gamma log(|U|+1) cancels its negative private rank term, leaving exactly gamma log(r+1). For the inside root the private rank cost is gamma log((r+1)/(|U|+1)) and congestion is zero. Empty-core roots also cost gamma log(r+1). Thus all private rank/congestion terms together are <=gamma log(r+1) I_data<=gamma I_core: use log(r+1)<=r, m 2^-m<=1/2 and b>=1/2.

Core-only labels cancel against log m actual-root entropy. On every other set, the remaining label cost minus posterior entropy is at most |T| log m; the -log(t!) saving stays nonpositive. Therefore it is <=I_data log m=2p m q log m<=4p<=4 I_core. For the middle bound log m<=m and m^2/2^m<=2 suffice: verify m=1,2,3 and use the ratio ((m+1)/m)^2/2<1 for m>=3. I_core>=rp>=p.

The complete residual, including all subset congestion, M=1 order costs, ranks, root labels, rarity and actual-run entropy, thus obeys

    R <= (4+2gamma+log e) I_core+I_data < 8 I(f).

The strict numerical comparison requires no floating point: ln 2>5/8 follows already from the first term 2/3 of its positive atanh series; hence log e<8/5 and 4+12/5+log e<8. For m=1 the same bound is valid without any multiple-root or label charge.

For a paid scale prior choose lambda_l^(j)=2^-j/m and w_j=1/[(j+1)(j+2)] for j>=0. These weights telescope to one; each whole conditioned generator is a subprobability. Selecting j=0 pays exactly v bits because w_0=1/2, and v<=2I_core/r<=2I. The complete residual plus this explicit prior is <=10I. This is an upper certificate at a feasible global vector, not a claim that uniform weights are optimal in every r,m case.

### New lead direction: geometric sunflower weights and the affine-plane obstruction

The B02 notes appeared at 2026-10-11 22:45 UTC, after this stage began, and were read in full before final persistence. They identify PI-J (prime-implicant whole-set coding) as the stream target and direct A009 to the outer fractional expression eq:outerfractional. The following is a source-bound test of that existing mechanism, not a new broad candidate. A008's finished dictionary report was also read: its sunflower quotient uses a different generator, so its bound neither replaces nor contradicts this residual.

For the r=1 sunflower, assign a common-coordinate pivotal input to the first positive private literal. Then alpha_0=b=1-2^-m, q_{0,l}=2^-l/b for l=1..m; a private-coordinate derivative has alpha_l=2^-m and only gate l eligible, so q_{l,l}=1. Choose the global rational weights lambda_l=2^-l. The common incidence sum is 1-2^-m<1, and each private row is 2^-l<=1. Put a=11/48. The outer sum satisfies

    S_0=b^(-1/2) sum_{l=1}^m 2^(-13l/48)
        <=sqrt(2)/(2^(13/48)-1)<9,
    log S_l=(11/48)l.

The last strict bound is elementary: 13/48>1/4, (7/6)^4<2, and sqrt(2)<3/2. Thus the pressure P=(48/5)sum_j alpha_j log S_j is at most (192/5)b+(11/5)2^-m m(m+1)/2 <41 I, using m(m+1)2^-m<=3/2 and I>=1. The latter bound follows from m=1,2,3 and the recurrence ratio (m+2)/(2m)<=1 for m>=2. This removes the uniform-weight (11/48)log m contribution inside every log S_j. The source's C_0<41 and v log Z<=0 yield a coarse H<82I, already known for this Boolean class; the new directional fact is the bounded fractional pressure with one explicit global vector and a specified tie rule. No entropy H is an input to the weights.

For each prime power Q>=16, let the coordinates be the Q^2 points of the affine plane over F_Q. Define f_Q=1 iff some affine line has all its Q point bits positive; otherwise f_Q=-1. There are N=Q(Q+1) genuine conjunction gates and k=Q+1 gates incident to each point. All are prime implicants: if a positive pattern contains a line, its minimal positive subset is that entire line; a set of fewer than Q points cannot contain any line. Conversely every line is minimal positive. All gate supports are nonempty and distinct, each cover has M_l=1, and each pi_l=2^-Q.

Fix a point j and a line l through it. Conditional on all Q-1 other points of l being positive, an alternative incident line is ready with probability 2^(-(Q-1)), since its other points are disjoint from l. A nonincident line has at most one forced positive point, so its probability of being fully positive at the j-negative endpoint is at most the same quantity. A union bound over N-1 other lines gives probability at least 1-epsilon_Q that no such alternative occurs, where

    epsilon_Q=Q(Q+1)2^(-(Q-1))<1/2 for every integer Q>=16.

These clean events for different lines through j are disjoint and make j pivotal. Therefore

    alpha_j >= k 2^(-(Q-1))(1-epsilon_Q)>0.

For ANY fixed disjoint pointwise tie decomposition of this derivative among pivotal line gates, each piece's squared norm is at most 2^(-(Q-1)). Hence its normalized mass obeys q_{j,l}<=1/[k(1-epsilon_Q)]<=2/k. These are precisely the disjoint signed pieces with masses summing to one required by the source outerfractional formula.

Every feasible global lambda has sum_{l incident j}lambda_l<=1. With a=11/48 and beta=1/[2(1+a)]=24/59, Holder gives the local relaxation bound

    S_j=sum_l sqrt(q_{j,l})lambda_l^-a
        >=(sum_l q_{j,l}^beta)^(1+a)
        >=(k/2)^(35/48).

The first inequality follows by applying Holder to q_l^beta=(sqrt(q_l)lambda_l^-a)^(1/(1+a)) lambda_l^(a/(1+a)). The second uses sum q=1 and q_l<=2/k, so sum q_l^beta>= (2/k)^(beta-1). This uses only the coordinate row, and thus holds for ALL simultaneous global incidence vectors; zeros with positive piece mass give infinite S_j rather than evade the bound. Consequently

    (48/5)sum_j alpha_j log S_j >= 7 I(f_Q) log((Q+1)/2).

Uniform lambda_l=1/k is feasible. Since sum sqrt(q_{j,l})<=sqrt k, it also gives pressure <=7I log k. Thus the optimized positive pressure grows logarithmically, between these two explicit bounds. No global weight choice repairs it. In addition v log Z cannot cancel that divergence: D=product_l(1+pi_l lambda_l)-1, log Z alone may diverge if weights shrink, but the positive pressure then rises; for the statement here only the nonnegative pressure requested by the lead is being bounded below. No claim about the infimum of the FULL expression with v log Z is inferred from this pressure lower bound. Its exact remaining cancellation is an additional question if one attempts to use this outer theorem rather than the whole-set forest mechanism.

This resolves the lead's requested O(I) pressure test negatively for the affine-plane DNF. The obstruction is the almost uniform distribution of genuinely pivotal line witnesses at each point, producing at least (35/48)log((Q+1)/2) in log S_j even after a local fractional relaxation. It does not disprove FEI, PI-J, or the posterior forest cost bound: all retain whole-set label sharing absent from this coordinatewise coarsening. Analytic finite-geometry incidence and a union bound establish the all-Q obstruction; no exponentially large truth-table computation is claimed.

### Exact remaining bridge

The full expectation is paid for this representation family. No claim that every Boolean function has such a representation is made. For arbitrary overlapping base gates in a shared core activation, the root component still cancels, but the base label/rank/congestion expectation need not have the binomial/private-singleton law used above. That is the first unsupported line for a universal whole-set cost theorem. The optimization dual pays no such missing structural expectation by itself.

# A009 stage 2: affine-plane prime-implicant forest payment

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Primary packet A009, stream B02, bridge C020 / PI-J. All entropies and log are base two, except ln explicitly. For each prime power q>=16, let f_q on q^2 independent fair point bits equal +1 if some affine line is entirely positive, and -1 otherwise. The gates are ALL its prime implicants, the N=q(q+1) width-q line conjunctions. Each point has read k=q+1. Fix lambda_l=1/k, pi_l=2^-q, one ordered cover per line (M_l=1), and any fixed order within each line. Every coordinate incidence row sums to one.

There exist deterministic actual partition-forest laws, fixed as a function of the spectral set but with a single global generator, whose complete conditioned residual satisfies

    R_q <= (186791/13540) I(f_q) < 14 I(f_q),  q>=16.

R includes v(Lambda+log Z), labels once per used gate, all root rarity, rank discrepancies, subset congestion, and actual-law entropy (zero here). This is an explicit upper bound for PI-J on this family, NOT an optimized residual value. It proves that the stage-1 all-weight coordinatewise pressure lower bound is not an obstruction to PI-J itself on the affine-plane family. No posterior entropy is needed for this upper certificate. The whole-set law can do better than the coordinatewise coarsening even with a point-mass posterior.

We also give a reusable equal-width linear-hypergraph cost-transfer inequality. The affine-plane application is its exact quantitative certificate, not another reproof of the B03 projective-plane FEI theorem. Full FEI and universal PI-J remain open; this scoped continuation awaits independent reconstruction.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### 1. Consume and independently reconstruct the retained-normalizer diagnostic

Write rho=2^-q, k=q+1, u=sum_l lambda_l, z_j=sum_{l through j}lambda_l, and P=(48/5)sum_j alpha_j log S_j. The stage-1 clean-pivotal-event bound gives normalized pivotal-piece mass <=2/k for every tie rule and all q>=16. Holder retaining the row mass gives

    P >=7I log(k/2)-(11/5)sum_j alpha_j log z_j.

Affine translation symmetry makes alpha_j constant. Double-counting incidences gives average_j z_j=u/q, hence Jensen implies

    P >=7I log(k/2)+(11/5)I log(q/u), 0<u<=q.

Let D=product(1+rho lambda_l)-1 and Z=D/(1+D). Expansion D>=rho u and monotonicity of x/(1+x) give

    log Z >=-q+log u-log(1+rho q).

The physical influence lower bound I>=2qN rho(1-epsilon), epsilon=2N rho<1/2, and the true-event union bound v<=4N rho give qv<=4I and v<=I. In particular v-(11/5)I<0. After adding the preceding inequalities, the scale-dependent coefficient of log u is negative, so its minimum is at u=q. Therefore

    P+v log Z >=7I log(k/2)+v log q-qv-v log(1+rho q)
               >=[7log(k/2)-5]I.

This proves A007's retained-normalizer clause independently, with no repair, at the exact stated scope. It rules out a coordinatewise rescue, including arbitrarily small scales; it does not address the actual whole-set forest label count. That is the first missing term connecting the previous lower bound to PI-J. We now pay that term for the affine-plane family.

### 2. A fixed actual-forest rule

Fix all line orders globally. If a nonempty spectral set A is contained in a line, choose the first such line and assign ALL of A to it. The run has one used gate, one root, no children, and s=kappa=|A|, so subset congestion is zero. For |A|>=2 the containing line is unique. For singletons any fixed first-line rule is sufficient.

Otherwise assign every point j in A to its first incident line. Discard empty owners, form the directed incidence graph l->l' when A_l intersects R_l', choose the least gate in every source strongly connected component, and grow a deterministic directed spanning forest from these roots. Choose the first allowed attachment point on every edge and a deterministic child draw order. A source-SCC root reaches every gate in its component, and every component is reached from some source in the finite condensation DAG; so this produces a finite forest visiting every used gate once. Its selected subsets partition A. Positive lambda and the incidence rows guarantee positive prescribed child-label probabilities. The uniform multiset and subset choices in the source generator also give positive probability. Thus this is an actual terminating queue run, not an abstract cover counted as a successful run.

The prime-implicant table is exact: every positive input contains a line, and a minimal positive support must be exactly that line. Uniform width and distinct lines exclude proper containment between line gates. There are no constant, empty-support, or duplicate gates. For the chosen point-mass law H(eta_A)=0; any improvement by averaging actual runs is optional, not required.

### 3. Ideal union amplitudes and a paid spectral error

Let b_l be the positive line indicator, K(x)=sum_l b_l(x), f=2*1[K>=1]-1, and G=2K-1. G is a real approximation, not a Boolean function; we do not apply FEI or any entropy inequality to G. Put e=f-G. Pointwise,

    e(x)^2=4(K-1)_+^2 <=4K(K-1).

Distinct affine lines meet in at most one point. Therefore E(b_l b_l')<=2^(1-2q) for l!=l', giving the exact Parseval error bound

    E2:=||e||_2^2 <=8N(N-1)4^-q <=8N^2 4^-q.

For nonempty A, |Ghat(A)|=2rho times the number of lines containing A. Its sign is the common conjunction-character sign; the binary checker uses (-1)^|A|. Hence Ghat vanishes on all off-line sets. On sets of size >=2 contained in a line there is exactly one ideal amplitude. The only coherent overlap is at singletons, where its multiplicity is k. No incoherent spectral law is substituted for the actual f law.

On good sets contained in a line, write gamma=6/5 and

    F_l(A)=gamma sum_{j in A} log[r_l(j)/(|A intersect predecessors_l(j)|+1)].

Let D=(1+rho/k)^N-1, a=N rho/k=q rho. Expansion and exp(a)-1<=a exp(a) give

    q+log D+log k = log N+delta =: c,
    0<=delta<=a log e.

Here the root rarity q and the negative normalization have been retained together; c>=0. Every good set's EXACT conditioned residual cost is C_A=c+F_l(A). For q>=16, D<1, since D<=a exp(a)<2a<1, with a<=1/4096. The conditioning term is negative but has already paid the good-set root rarity before it is bounded.

For one conjunction with a fixed order, a uniformly chosen subset selects each rank with probability 1/2. Conditional on selection of rank j, its predecessor count is Bin(j-1,1/2), and E[1/(1+Bin)]=2(1-2^-j)/j. Jensen gives conditional log rank at most one, hence the uniform-subset mean of F_l is <=gamma q/2. This elementary binomial estimate was used in stage 1; we reuse it, rather than claiming a new rank lemma.

It follows by summing the ideal amplitudes over each line, and separately upper-bounding coherent singleton amplitudes, that

    sum_good Ghat(A)^2 <=4N rho+4q^2 k^2 rho^2,
    sum_good Ghat(A)^2 F_selected(A)
        <=2gamma Nq rho+4gamma q^2 k^2 log q rho^2.

For the second inequality, sets of size >=2 have a unique line; the sum over all single-line subsets already controls their rank bill. Add the entire singleton ideal mass, at rank cost at most gamma log q. This deliberately double-counts some singleton rank mass as an upper bound, avoiding any silent cancellation of shared amplitudes.

### 4. Transfer the full cost to the actual Boolean spectrum

For every good frequency, fhat^2<=(2Ghat^2+2ehat^2). All good costs C_A are nonnegative. If C_max bounds their maximum, summation gives

    good residual <=2 sum_good Ghat(A)^2 C_A+2 C_max E2.

For bad/off-line A, Ghat(A)=0 so fhat(A)^2=ehat(A)^2, and their entire mass is <=E2. A generic actual forest has at most |A| used gates/roots, kappa_l<=q, and ranks <=q. Thus

    B_lambda(A) <= |A|[q+log k+2gamma log q]
                 <=q^2[q+log k+2gamma log q]=:B_max.

This pays gate labels, root rarity, subset congestion and ranks together, not just a rank-tail statistic. Its cover multiplicity term is zero because M=1. Since log D<0, each bad set's complete cost log D+B is at most B, so

    bad residual <=B_max E2.

The spectral error is used once in the two good/bad subcollections; their upper bounds may conservatively each use the full E2, as displayed. No credit is claimed twice as an exact identity.

For integer q>=16, log q<=q/4. Indeed equality holds at 16, and 2^(1/4)>7/6>17/16>=(q+1)/q makes 2^(q/4)>=q propagate to successive integers. Also log k<=1+log q<=5q/16 and log N<=1+2log q<=9q/16. The positive atanh series gives log e<8/5, so delta<=q/16. Therefore

    c<=5q/8,
    C_max <=c+gamma q log q <=q^2/2,
    B_max <=(153/80)q^3 <2q^3.

Combining these bounds and the ideal expectations gives the complete source residual upper bound

    R <= (49/5)Nq rho+(37/5)q^3 k^2 rho^2
         +8N^2(q^2+2q^3)rho^2.                         (U)

In particular the used-gate label bill is small because one gate owns the typical entire spectral set; the common read k is not charged separately to each point. The rare off-line mass pays every worst-case root/label/rank charge through its explicit E2 bound.

### 5. Analytic all-parameter certificate

Fix a point j and one incident line. Conditional on its q-1 other points being positive, every other incident line is ready with probability rho*2. Every nonincident line is fully positive at the j-negative endpoint with probability at most 2rho, since it contains at most one forced point. The union bound excludes all alternative lines with failure at most epsilon=2N rho. Clean events for the k incident lines are disjoint, so

    alpha_j >=2k rho(1-epsilon),
    I(f_q)>=2Nq rho(1-epsilon)>0.

This is a physical influence calculation, not an entropy or surrogate lower bound. Divide (U) by that lower bound to obtain

    R/I <= [49/5+(37/5)N rho+8N(q+2q^2)rho]/[2(1-2N rho)]. (C)

For q>=16, every positive-polynomial numerator term divided by 2^q decreases: N has degree two and N(q+2q^2) has degree at most four, with nonnegative coefficients; its successive ratio is bounded by ((q+1)/q)^4/2<1. The denominator increases since N/2^q decreases. Thus (C) is maximized at q=16, where exact rational arithmetic yields

    (C)|_{q=16}=186791/13540,
    14-186791/13540=2769/13540>0.

The whole derivation uses only affine-plane incidence, valid over every finite field, and integer inequalities valid for all q>=16. No extrapolation from q=2 or 3 is used. This proves the stated uniform PI-J certificate on an unbounded family. Small q data below serve as checks of the actual forest generator only; those small cubes are not in this sparse asymptotic regime.

### 6. Reusable linear-hypergraph certificate and exact universal gap

For any distinct positive width-w conjunctions on n covered variables with pairwise support intersection <=1, let N be gate count, k maximum read, rho=2^-w, uniform lambda=1/k, D=(1+rho/k)^N-1 and c=w+log D+log k>=log N>=0. Choose the same forest rule, using one gate whenever A lies in a support and otherwise a source-SCC forest. The entire preceding transfer argument gives

    R <=2{c[4N rho+4n k^2 rho^2]+2gamma Nw rho
             +4gamma n k^2 log w rho^2}
        +8N^2 rho^2{2[c+gamma w log w]
             +n[w+log k+2gamma log w]+max(0,log D)}.      (T)

This is a scalar, verifiable upper certificate, not an assertion that it is O(I) for every linear hypergraph. The extra max(0,log D) retains the possibility of a positive normalizer on generic dense inputs, while the affine certificate had log D<0. Width w=1 has log w=0; distinct singleton gates introduce no ambiguous duplicate columns. A clean-event lower bound I>=2Nw rho(1-2N rho) applies when 2N rho<1; otherwise this particular proof gives no useful influence lower bound.

The precise unpaid universal line is controlling the error-cost budget in (T) by I in arbitrary density and overlap regimes. When N2^-w is not small, the Boolean union can differ substantially from the sum of indicators; our crude E2 times worst-case forest cost may dominate I. Nothing here proves that every Boolean function has a prime-implicant table with small perturbation error. B03/A014 can consume (T) as a full-label/subset/root interface for their linear-hypergraph work. Broad arbitrary-density linear-hypergraph PI-J, overlapping intersections beyond one point, and universal FEI remain open.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

Both fixed orders make all size/subset factors rational and symmetric. Matching multiplicity cancels the growing label cost. ## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

WEIGHT_GAP.md gives the full proof. Complete unnormalized star mass W_A is multiaffine in lambda because each actual event visits each gate once. Frozen usage c_e is lambda_e times its exact mass derivative, averaged against the TRUE Boolean spectrum. The complete objective is K+v ln D-sum c_e ln lambda_e, with the actual subset/count/entropy constant K retained. Every c_e>0 by the positive singleton derivative sector. For >=2 edges, a three-/four-variable restriction implies a positive higher-degree Fourier set, hence total usage C>v. This yields boundary coercivity and an attained positive optimum; full affine span of the nonempty root indicators yields strict convexity and uniqueness in log weights. The one-edge case is explicitly constant, not unique.

Any root law with b=c-vr>=0 and coordinate prices gives the full primal/dual gap as root KL plus incidence slack plus scalar edge relative entropy. The singleton root law c/C always supplies admissible marginals. Mixing it with the actual normalized nonempty root law repairs negative b rationally. The FULL KL cost is bounded by the exact root chi-square of this mixture, whose closed form needs only singleton root probabilities; no root/SCC independence shortcut is used. Scalar edge gaps are bounded by squared stationarity errors. Thus twice the rational natural gap gives a rigorous bit gap using ln2>1/2, without evaluating logarithms or trusting a numerical optimum.

For 68 frozen graph posteriors, rounding the numerical proposed weights downward to denominator 10^8 preserves every incidence row exactly. # A009: complete fractional-incidence payment with arbitrary cores

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

For every finite simple graph H on r>=1 hubs and every m_i>=1, attach m_i private degree-one leaves at each hub, retaining all core edges. Prove a complete nonempty reference cross-entropy bound on the actual uniform Boolean edge DNF, in bits, using one global positive packing-feasible vector on precisely the original primes. The result is C_PL <= [log2 96+9/4+9/(8 ln2)] I < (843/80) I <11I. Core degree, core density, hub count and unequal leaf counts are unrestricted. This is a class theorem beyond bounded-read formulas, not a universal FEI solution or a global-weight optimality assertion.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

The execution protocol is 2026-10-11-execution-v2. ## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

PRIVATE_LEAF_PAYMENT.md contains the full proof and all-dimensional inequalities. Each private edge at hub i receives 1/(2m_i), and each core edge ij receives 1/[2 max(d_H(i),d_H(j))]. Hub incidence is at most one and leaf incidence at most one half. The actual chosen events use private gates; every unused core gate remains in the nonempty-root normalizer D. Exact chronological stars give block numerators U_0(t)=(1/(64m))^t, U_1(0)=1/64 and U_1(t)=t! (1/(2m))^t/[24*8^(t-1)*t^2*(t+1)]. These imply Q_PL(A)>=D^-1 96^-|A| product_i m_i^-t_i.

Write q=Pr(false), a_i=Pr(false,hub_i negative), b_i=Pr(false,hub_i positive). Genuine private leaves give b_i<=2^-m_i a_i, Inf_hub_i=2(a_i-b_i)>=a_i and >=2q/3, and each leaf has influence 2b_i. Thus all private label charges sum to at most (9/4) I. Total weight is at most 3r/4; v=4q(1-q) and I>=2rq/3 pay v log2 D by 9I/(8 ln2), including its negative sign when D<1. Adding the log2(96) I pointwise cost proves the displayed bound. Rational inequalities log2 96<33/5 and ln2>2/3 yield 843/80<11. The first unsupported extension is a same-reference replacement for the block numerator and b_i<=2^-m_i a_i when a vertex has zero private leaves or its neighbors are shared. Attaching leaves changes f and supplies no free transfer to the original core.

REVIEW_CONNECTED.md reconstructs the whole unchanged A008-CONNECTED-GRAPH-TWO-SCALE-QUANTIFIER-SEPARATION v1, hash cf0f06a75dabf85738b3cbfcfabc9142aa5cbbb9c62add74dfe300bcc59321f5: influence and singleton spectral mass, divergent uniform-scale complete/surrogate cost, exact repaired normalizer, and its sharper C_MS<(53/5)I for all m>=2. ## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

This is an UPPER bound on the unrestricted PI-J infimum, not a certificate over all continuous lambda, all covers/orders, or arbitrary representations. All entropies and residuals are in bits; I is total uniform-cube influence. Constants have H=I=0 and are excluded from ratios.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

EXECUTION_PROTOCOL.json revision 2026-10-11-execution-v2 was read in full. Read the README, current status, claim and obstruction registries, packet and stream navigation, campaign plan's endpoints/A010 acceptance contract, earlier B05/B09/B19 run reports, supplemental campaign/claims/counterexamples/mapping/seed material and source introductions.
