Its partition forest definition requires one root per source SCC and a directed spanning forest. Its proof only needs the support/ordered-cover parameter table for the posterior bound.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

For a fixed table define T=product_l(1+lambda_l*pi_l)-1=2^Lambda Z. For every nonempty spectral set A let W_A=sum_r 2^-B_lambda(A;r), summing distinct actual permitted partition-forest runs. Finite log-sum optimization gives inf_P(E_P B-H(P))=-log2 W_A, with minimizing posterior proportional to 2^-B. Consequently the complete optimized residual for this table is

R=sum_{A nonempty} fhat(A)^2 log2(T/W_A).

The factor T retains both activation normalization and nonempty-root conditioning. It is rational for conjunction gates. In particular it is not a sum of separately optimized root terms.

For assignment tau of each j in A to an incident gate, put B_l={j:tau(j)=l}, s_l=|B_l|, kappa_l=|A intersect R_l|, and list R_l in increasing physical coordinate order. Let a_{lj}=|A intersect strict predecessors of j|+1 and r_{lj} its rank. With M_l=1 and gamma=6/5, the run weight is exactly

2^-B = (product_used lambda_l)(product_roots 2^-|R_l|) D_tau^(6/5),

where D_tau=product_used(s_l/kappa_l)^s_l times product_assigned(a_{lj}/r_{lj}). This establishes the rational fifth-root interface used by the code.

Primary enumeration: construct the assignment graph l->k iff B_l intersects R_k. Enumerate every parent map with roots exactly one vertex per source SCC and no directed cycle. Enumerate every connecting coordinate for each edge. If a parent has c_j children attached through coordinate j, the queue has exactly product_j c_j! distinct child-label sequences: occurrences of distinct connecting coordinates have deterministic order; distinct child labels at an equal coordinate can be permuted. Sum this multiplicity. Assignments, parent maps, attachments and these sequences recover a unique actual queue trace; conversely each allowed partition trace recovers these data. Root order is fixed and unused gates are never visited. Thus no interchangeability entropy is omitted or counted twice.

NPN transport: input flips preserve Fourier squares and support incidence. An input permutation transports each ordered support along with the truth table. Hence a representative's table supplies an admissible table for every member of its orbit. Lexicographic gate orders on separate truth tables need not themselves be permutation invariant. Output complementation is evaluated explicitly and leaves H,I unchanged. The recorded chosen orientation supplies an existence upper bound, even when two orientation intervals overlap.

Exact certificates: Walsh coefficients are integers divided by 2^n; Parseval and a separate directed cube-edge influence count are asserted for every evaluated orientation. Prime conjunctions are identified by truth-cell containment and verified to union to the exact positive set. D^(6/5) is enclosed between adjacent 72-bit dyadics using integer fifth-power comparisons. For log x, scale to 1<=y<2 and use ln y=2 sum_{j>=0} z^(2j+1)/(2j+1), z=(y-1)/(y+1)<=1/3. After forty terms, the omitted tail is at most 2 z^81/[81(1-z^2)]. All intermediate nonnegative quantities are rounded outward to 80-bit dyadics; logarithm endpoints are rounded outward to 64 bits. Negative exponents and signed interval division use correctly ordered bounds and all four quotient corners. Rational inequalities comparing resulting endpoints are certificates, while floating midpoint sorting is only a convenience.

Weight-boundary handoff (analytic, for any finite fixed admissible support/order table): W_A(lambda) is a polynomial with nonnegative coefficients (algebraic for the rational prime-conjunction tables here), multilinear in the gate weights, since each gate is visited at most once. T(lambda)=product_l(1+lambda_l*pi_l)-1 is also multilinear with positive coefficients. Fix a positive packing vector mu and let lambda=t mu, 0<t<=1. Let d_A be the minimum number of gate supports needed to cover A. Any such minimum cover can be assigned nonempty pieces (otherwise it was redundant), and its assignment graph has a permitted source-root spanning forest. Conversely every run's used supports cover A. Therefore W_A(t mu)=c_A t^d_A(1+O(t)) with c_A>0. Also T(t mu)=t sum_l mu_l*pi_l+O(t^2). For the fixed finite Fourier law, exact logarithmic expansion gives

R(t mu)=D log2(1/t)+O(1), where D=sum_{A nonempty} P_f(A)(d_A-1)>=0.

The O(1) depends on the fixed table and direction; no uniformity over changing directions or dimensions is asserted. Thus uniform scaling to the all-zero boundary cannot lower the infimum when some positive-mass A requires two supports: it forces R to +infinity. If D=0, the ray has a finite limit determined by the leading coefficients, not necessarily divergence. This does not exclude a partial-weight boundary, or approaches whose direction changes with t. In particular one must still certify those faces when claiming failure of an infimum. The actual two-literal OR has d_{singleton}=1, d_{pair}=2 and pair Fourier mass 1/4, so D=1/4; its exact formula below independently confirms that coefficient. This finite-polynomial observation is a narrow optimizer handoff, not a new broad FEI candidate.

The complete bounded-class infimum is closed analytically for n<=2, allowing BOTH output orientations, EVERY positive admissible weight and EVERY full-support single-cover order for each prime conjunction. NPN leaves four types: constant, dictator, point defect and two-bit parity. A dictator has R=0 by single-root cancellation. A point's minority table has one width-two conjunction; lambda cancels, and the four Fourier squares are 1/4. Only the later singleton has rank charge 6/5, so R=3/10 and I=1, regardless of the two possible orders. The other output orientation has two disjoint literal primes with independent weights a,b in (0,1]. Put u=a+b, v=ab and T=u/2+v/4. Direct singleton/two-root masses give R=(3/4)log2 T-(1/2)log2 v+1. For fixed v, u>=2 sqrt(v) makes R smallest at a=b=t=sqrt(v). Then R=1-(1/4)log2 t+(3/4)log2(1+t/4), strictly decreasing on 0<t<=1 (derivative sign proportional to -1+ t/2). Its minimum is 1+(3/4)log2(5/4)>1>3/10. Thus choosing the minority table is the TRUE infimum for this bounded class, not merely a uniform-weight upper bound.

For two-bit parity both prime gates have the full support and pi=1/4. The only spectral set is A={1,2}. For arbitrary a,b>0 with a+b<=1, T=u/4+v/16 and W=u/4+v*2^(-12/5). Hence R=log2[(1+v/(4u))/(1+4*2^(-12/5)*v/u)]. Since 4*2^(-12/5)=2^(-2/5)>1/4, this decreases in v/u. The exact inequality v/u<=u/4<=1/4 gives the global minimum at a=b=1/2, for either output orientation and all gate orders. The exact ratio is (1/2)log2[(17/16)/(1+2^(-12/5))], about -0.081424729644. All boundary limits are included by these monotonic bounds. Constants are handled with R=I=0. Therefore the maximum TRUE bounded-class PI-J infimum divided by I on n<=2 is exactly 3/10. This closes continuous weights and all orders only in the declared all-prime, one-full-order-per-gate class; other representations are outside this claim.

The first unsupported universal line is R_PI-J(f)<=K I(f) for all dimensions with an absolute K, after optimizing one global lambda and the permitted order table. Nothing in the n=3,4 enumeration supplies that line or a lower bound on the lambda infimum there.

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Full FEI and all-dimensional PI-J remain OPEN. The stage follows Claude B02 lead v2's instruction to add the single-gate L2 table to the n<=4 prime-table census. Entropy is in bits, P_f(A)=fhat(A)^2, I=sum_A |A|P_f(A), gamma=6/5. The residual excludes h2(Var f) and K_ref I: it is precisely eq:foresttarget, optimized over conditional distributions on DISTINCT actual partition forests.

Define the frozen representation menu M(f): (S) one gate h=f or -f, one FULL order of its relevant support, any positive admissible weight; (P) exactly ALL prime implicants of either output side, one FULL support order per prime conjunction, any positive globally fixed weights satisfying all incidence constraints. No extra gates, duplicate gates, multiple covers, partial support covers, or unrestricted nonpartition successful runs are in this menu. Each table's posterior is optimized exactly. Limiting zero-weight faces are included when analyzing the infimum, but actual admissible tables have positive weights. Constants are assigned R=I=0.

The exact finite result is

sup_{nonconstant f, n<=4} inf_{table in M(f)} R(f;table)/I(f) = alpha4 = (43+19 log2 3)/80 = 0.9139285939212746... .

Equality holds exactly on the 96 four-bit NPN images of OR(AND2,AND2). For every other four-bit function the optimal single-table ratio is at least 1/10 below alpha4, so its menu infimum is also strictly lower. This closes the worst-case variational constant of this declared bounded menu, NOT each function's individual prime-only infimum, arbitrary representations, or the all-dimensional conjecture.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

**Single gate and local independent review of L1/L2.** For N=1, M=1 and any lambda>0, T=2^Lambda Z=lambda*pi, and every nonempty A has one partition forest with label-plus-root term log2(1/(lambda*pi)). It cancels log2 T. Thus lambda drops out exactly, including its limiting zero boundary. In a fixed full order, selected predecessor counts are 0,...,|A|-1, giving product_{j in A}(|A intersect predecessors(j)|+1)=|A|!. Therefore

R_S=(6/5)[sum_j Inf_j log2 r(j)-E log2 |A|!].

Swapping adjacent influences u<v at ranks r<r+1 changes the rank sum by (u-v)log2((r+1)/r)<0. Repeated swaps prove the optimum is decreasing influence order, with no floating optimization. For a general ordered M-cover, one root still cancels and the deterministic minimum-forest upper bound leaves I log2 M plus gamma/ln2 times the minimum rank cost; this reconstructs L1. This M-cover expression is a sufficient upper bound, not an identity for its entropy-optimized posterior. The full all-n statements L1 and L2 are independently accepted locally at their stated scope, conditional only on the cited posterior-forest theorem; their source records are in out/reviews.json. O1 was not re-audited or re-proved.

**Finite single-gate upper certificate.** On four bits only primes 2 and 3 occur in logs of ranks and factorials. If W_A are the integer Walsh coefficients, let J_j=sum_{A containing j} W_A^2 and L_s=sum_{|A|=s} W_A^2, with J_(1)>=...>=J_(4). Then

R_S/I = [c2+c3 log2 3]/d,
c2=6[J_(2)+2J_(4)-(L_2+L_3+3L_4)],
c3=6[J_(3)-(L_3+L_4)], d=5 sum_j J_j.

raw_rank.py exhausts every truth mask with an independent Walsh popcount formula and checks influences by edge masks. The strict inequality to alpha4 is the rational-interval sign of (43d-80c2)+(19d-80c3)log2 3. Exact equality is checked by both integer coefficients vanishing. The exhaustive equality masks coincide with the independently generated NPN orbit of truth 855. Dummy-coordinate padding covers every n<4. Separately, single_compare.py checks 238 prior orbit representatives, all 5128 relevant full orders, and direct rank sums against the closed expression as prime-exponent coefficient maps. These checks also reproduce the prior prime-table baseline for comparison without re-running its expensive forest census.

**Matching witness: single gate.** For f=OR of two disjoint width-two conjunctions, after input flips/relabeling, all four influences are 3/8, I=3/2, v=63/64. The spectrum has empty mass 1/64, six sets confined to one original block with mass 9/64 each, and nine sets meeting both blocks with mass 1/64 each. The single-gate coefficient map is exactly 43/80 + (19/80)log2 3. All full orders have the same value because the influences are equal; it is attained for every positive single weight.

**Matching witness: positive prime side, ALL weights/orders.** The two positive primes are disjoint conjunctions, pi=1/4. Every spectral set has a unique assignment, and every used block is a root. Each block is used with probability 9/16. All four choices of within-block full orders have the same expected rank cost by within-block symmetry. For weights a,b in (0,1],

R(a,b)=(63/64)log2 T-(9/16)log2(ab)+27/10,
T=(a+b)/4+ab/16.

At fixed ab, a+b>=2 sqrt(ab), and T increases with a+b, so the minimum is at a=b=t. Differentiating the t-dependent part gives, up to a positive factor, -72+54t, strictly negative on 0<t<=1. Thus the exact minimum is at a=b=1. Its ratio is beta=(21/16)log2 3-33/40=1.2552632821965175... . Since log2 3>3/2 (9>8), beta>1. This argument includes all limiting boundaries and all orders.

**Matching witness: complementary prime side, ALL weights/orders.** The four complementary primes are the four choices of one falsified literal in each original block; their support graph is K2,2, each pi=1/4. All 16 full support-order tables are explicitly included. For each nonempty spectral A, the exact mass polynomial is

W_A(lambda)=sum_{U,d} c_{A,U,d} lambda_U z^d, z=2^(-6/5), lambda_U=product_{l in U} lambda_l,

with nonnegative rational c. Every gate is visited once, so no weight exponent exceeds one. T(lambda)=product_l(1+lambda_l/4)-1 is also multiaffine. Coefficients retain source-SCC root restrictions and distinct child sequences. prime_poly.py extracts them by assignment/parent maps; verify_continuum.py independently reconstructs all 300 set/table polynomials by actual queue growth and deduplicated traces, using a separate reachability/SCC implementation.

For a fixed positive-denominator coordinate box, W_A/T is a ratio of multiaffine polynomials. Holding other coordinates fixed makes it (a x+b)/(c x+d), whose derivative has constant sign. Repeated endpoint replacement shows its maximum occurs at a box vertex. The only zero-denominator vertex is the all-zero vector. The possible limiting values there are bounded by the largest one-gate leading coefficient divided by pi; if A requires two gates, this limiting mass is zero. The same bound follows along coordinate rays and positive mixtures of them. Hence every box, including boxes meeting origin and partial-zero faces, has a rigorous vertex upper bound m_A on W_A/T. Coefficient rounding is only upward, so the bound remains valid.

For every such box,
R/I >= -sum_{A nonempty} [P_f(A)/I(f)] log2 m_A.

A finite binary splitting cover, with packing-infeasible boxes excluded only when their LOWER endpoints already violate a constraint, proves R/I>=1 for every complementary table and feasible positive weight vector. Continuity/lower-limit arguments retain all zero faces; any face with missing spectral output has infinite cost. The 16 splitting trees contain 156 nodes and 86 leaves in total. A separate checker verifies Kraft sum one, prefix-free leaf paths, the decoded dyadic boxes, all packing exclusions, and the lower bounds. It uses a separately chosen rational z_upper=435276/1000000, checking z_upper^5>=1/64 by integers, and a different twelve-term rational log series with proved remainder. Its smallest certified lower endpoint is >1.003981089. This checker does NOT import the certificate generator's vertex evaluator or logarithm implementation.

**Conclusion of menu infimum.** For the witness, S gives alpha4<1 (log2 3<5/3, since 27<32); positive P gives beta>1; complementary P gives at least 1. Thus EVERY admissible menu representation, weight and order has ratio >=alpha4, and S attains equality. The finite upper census supplies alpha4 for every Boolean function through four bits. Together these establish the exact supremum of the menu infimum. The 96-element equality family is a known read-once calibration; no new FEI class or all-dimensional bound is claimed.

**Reusable quantifier tool, not a new FEI estimate.** For any fixed finite partition-forest table, W_A and T are multiaffine in global lambda. Positive coefficient enclosures and ratio vertex bounds therefore permit globally valid weight certificates WITHOUT assuming joint convexity of the posterior objective (A009's counterexample is respected). Finite box certification need not terminate at a sharp or zero-margin threshold; no completeness or polynomial-time guarantee is claimed. This supplies a mechanism for certifying a whole weight domain instead of rejecting one representation.

The first unsupported universal line remains sup_n sup_f inf_{prime tables} R/I<infinity. The prime-only n<=4 sharp infimum constant is not established, and allowing additional gate covers or arbitrary representations changes the infimum. The separate pure-prime bracket below addresses that infimum without identifying its sharp value.

**Pure-prime continuation: whole-domain P4 lower bound.** The rational grid only supplies a chosen-table upper bound: on truth863, gates with masks 6,9,12, orders (2,1),(3,0),(2,3), weights (7/8,7/8,1/8), the certified upper endpoint is below 130101/100000 (midpoint 1.3010094884). Independently of this search, p4_continuum.py covers ALL weights, both output orientations, and all 16 full gate-order tables with the same multiaffine-ratio argument. p4_verify.py reconstructs all spectral polynomials by the separate queue algorithm, verifies a prefix-free complete dyadic cover, and uses its separate rational logarithm/fifth-root bounds. Every table has R/I>=32/25. This is a lower bound on the variational infimum, not a failure of one representation. The source-bound stage-1 chosen-table intervals for every OTHER NPN class are strictly below 32/25, as rechecked exactly in out/pure_prime_bracket.json. Consequently the pure-prime n<=4 worst infimum belongs precisely to the P4 NPN class and lies in [32/25,130101/100000]. Its sharp value remains unproved. P4 has read two, so repeating bounded-degree paths cannot itself give an unbounded route obstruction; the next growing family must have increasing read or a different unpaid mechanism.

# A011 / B03: representation existence

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

For every finite selector decision tree on an independent uniform selector
cube, querying each selector coordinate at most once along each path,
and every collection of Boolean leaves on a common uniform data cube,
construct a gate cover and a complete whole-spectrum witness. Shared
variables, repeated leaf functions and colliding Fourier supports are
allowed. Entropy and code lengths are in bits; influence is the usual
uniform physical edge-flip probability summed over coordinates.

Let d_ell be leaf depth, p_ell=2^-d_ell, and D=sum p_ell d_ell. If each leaf
has a complete spectral witness code with expected length c_ell, the
constructed full code has expected length exactly

    2D + sum p_ell c_ell.

If I_sel(F)>=gamma D and c_ell<=K I(g_ell), its cost is at most
max(2/gamma,K) I(F). This is a genuinely explicit admission condition.
For unsigned nonempty character leaves, c_ell=0. Defining B as the expected
number of selector flips changing the character label gives I_sel=B/2.
The named class B>=beta D has a fully paid cost <=(4/beta) I(F).

For singleton characters the positive event is a union of ordinary
conjunction gates. The alternating two-label comb has beta=1/2,
unbounded worst-case read, repeated data labels, and I_sel/a tending to
zero. Its complete cost is <=8 I_sel and hence <=8 I(F).

AUTHOR_PROVED_PENDING_INDEPENDENT_REVIEW. Full representation existence
for every Boolean function with an absolute paid constant: OPEN.
Full FEI: OPEN. Novelty and public-release status: NOT CLAIMED.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

The complete standalone derivation is STRUCTURED_COVER.tex. Its critical
steps are reproduced here for reviewers.

At a selector node with actual Boolean children G,H and residual Fourier
coefficients u_T,v_T, set q_T=(u_T^2+v_T^2)/2 and
theta_T=(u_T-v_T)^2/(4q_T). Choose a fair physical child, then its recursive
spectral witness with decoded set T, then a Bernoulli spectral bit Z with
parameter theta_T. The decoded parent probabilities are exactly

    q_T(1-theta_T)=(u_T+v_T)^2/4,
    q_T theta_T=(u_T-v_T)^2/4.

Both are the actual squared parent Fourier coefficients. The Bernoulli
law is valid by (u-v)^2<=2(u^2+v^2). Zero-q packets are never sampled.
This recursive construction retains the signs and all coherent cross
terms. It does not replace the Boolean children by normalized real
mixture components or use a false entropy-subadditivity claim.

The physical leaf path has probability 2^-d. Encode that prefix (d bits),
the complete leaf witness prefix, then exactly d selector-frequency bits
along the known queried path. Its Kraft sum is

    sum_ell 2^-d_ell [sum_w 2^-L_leaf(w)] 2^d_ell 2^-d_ell <= 1.

It is prefix-free and uniquely decodes the witness: physical paths are
prefixes ending at leaves; each leaf dictionary is prefix-free; the final
block length is fixed by the decoded path. Output spectral coordinates
are recovered from the leaf set and final membership bits. The true law
need not have uniform spectral bits, and their entire cost is still paid
at one bit each. Conditional physical leaf witnesses retain the original
leaf law because all later spectral innovations sum to one.

Let W be this exact witness and S its decoded output. Then
H(S)<=H(W)<=E L(W)=2D+sum p c. Pushing Kraft masses through the decoder
gives a complete subprobability reference Q(S); for every sampled w,
-log2 Q(S(w))<=L(w). Thus the full cross-entropy is also paid. Multiple
witnesses decoding to the same S are summed, not discarded or counted
as distinct output states. Their posterior entropy is present in
H(S)=H(W)-H(W|S).

The explicit cover is C_ell AND [g_ell=1], where C_ell is the conjunction
of the path literals. For character leaves, parity is itself a Boolean
combination of coordinate regular gates; singleton characters give
ordinary conjunction gates immediately. Finite truth tables provide an
algorithm for all child coefficients and witness transitions. Neither
efficient construction nor a short encoding of the static function's
dictionary is claimed or required by the reference interface.

Conditioning on selectors gives I_data=sum p I(leaf). The admission
I_sel>=gamma D pays the selector transcript, and the leaf codes pay only
data influence; the budget is never reused. Distinct unsigned characters
disagree with probability 1/2, proving I_sel=B/2 for that specialization.
Coordinates not fixed by the reached leaf stay in the same cell under
a flip, so B<=D. The condition B>=beta D is exact, physical, and checkable.

For the alternating-label comb, query j is reached with probability
2^-(j-1), so D=2(1-2^-a). Its continuation disagreement probabilities obey
r_a=1 and r_j=1-r_(j+1)/2, giving 1/2<=r_j<=1 and
B=sum 2^-(j-1)r_j>=D/2. Thus I_sel=B/2<1 while a grows. The cover has a+1
gates, each using x1, and the two data literals repeat without bound.

First unsupported universal line: an arbitrary Boolean selector with
shared/colliding leaf spectra need not have any representation with
I_sel>=gamma D for an absolute positive gamma. This line is not used in
the restricted theorem. For example, the first-positive comb with every
stopping leaf y1 and only its terminal leaf y2 has
D=2(1-2^-a) but B=a*2^-(a-1), so B/D tends to zero. The code can be
wasteful on a perfectly valid easy FEI function. Optimizing that cost
universally is a remaining obligation, not an inferred no-go for FEI.

This is a new complete reference interface in this run, not a claimed
bound on the original queue's variational minimum. Distinct-character
affine selectors are included as calibration only. Repeated labels and
low average selector energy are the stated extension of those baseline
hypotheses. Literature novelty remains unverified.

# A011 / B03 stage 2: repair the low-boundary comb output cost

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

For every a>=1, every first-positive selector comb with arbitrary stopping
and terminal labels in {chi_A,chi_B}, and every distinct nonempty data
character supports A,B on an independent uniform data cube, construct
a complete fixed output reference and bound its entire description cost
without the previous condition I_sel>=gamma D. Labels may repeat without
bound and A,B may share data coordinates. Entropy and costs are in bits;
influence is the ordinary uniform physical edge-flip sum.

Result: a normalized reference Q_F computed from physical suffix means
satisfies CE(P_F,Q_F)=H(F)<=C_* I_sel(F), where
C_*=4+2/(e ln 2)<41/8. A physical OUTPUT prefix code has expected length
<CE+1<=(41/8) I(F), with zero-length deterministic output allowed. This
is an output code and does not decode a physical leaf history.

The same fixed conjunction/parity gate cover is constructive for every
comb label string; singleton data characters give ordinary conjunctions.
This is a repair of the accepted first-stage transcript, consuming the
specific A012 objection and B03 lead target, not an unrelated new broad
candidate under the pending-claim cap. The original general transcript
remains valid at its original scope. Current repair: AUTHOR_PROVED,
independent review pending. Full FEI and universal paid representation
existence remain OPEN; literature novelty is not asserted.

Do not interpret this as a new FEI class or a better total H/I bound:
the old comb code already has 2D<4 and I_data>=1, hence cost<=4 I(F)
without boundary admission. The mathematical advance is the stronger
complete IDEAL output cost paid solely by selector influence and tending
to zero with its boundary, leaving data influence available for a later
shared-data transfer. Integer output rounding is kept separate.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

POSTERIOR_REPAIR.tex supplies the complete all-dimensional proof;
COMB_QUOTIENT.md records the result as soon as derived; COVER_COST.md
is the full reference ledger. The key equations follow.

Let sigma_j encode the two data labels. Define suffix means
m_(a+1)=sigma_(a+1), m_j=(sigma_j+m_(j+1))/2 and disagreement probability
r_j=(1-sigma_j m_(j+1))/2. Set p=(1+m_1)/2, q=1-p. These are fixed
physical probabilities, computable before sampling any Fourier set.

For nonempty selector T with max(T)=j, the Boolean label selector g has

    ghat(T)=2^(1-j) sigma_j r_j (-1)^(|T|-1).

Since F=(1+g)chi_A/2+(1-g)chi_B/2, the empty-selector output masses are
p^2,q^2. Every max-index-j group has 2^j atoms, each of mass
2^(-2j) r_j^2, giving total v_j=2^-j r_j^2. The complete reference first
chooses the empty atom or group j with these exact masses; within j,
choose one fair data label and a uniform subset of j-1 known predecessors.
The highest coordinate j is included. Its normalization is exactly
p^2+q^2+sum v_j=1. Zeros are treated continuously and unsampled.

No physical stopping leaf is transmitted. This is an explicit coherent
output reference calculated from signed suffix coefficients; it is not
an arbitrary spectral prior substituted without a cost proof. Its true
law agrees with P_F by the displayed Fourier calculation.

Two exact expressions for J=I_sel(F) pay the cost:

    J=sum_j 2^-j r_j=(1/2)sum_j(j+1)v_j.

The first is physical boundary counting; the second is the mean size
of the group-uniform selector subsets. With V=sum v_j=2pq, the full cost
is L(p^2)+L(q^2)+2sum jv_j+2sum v_j log2(1/r_j), where L(z)=z log2(1/z).
The binary correction L(p^2)+L(q^2)<=4pq=2V cancels the negative -2V
in 2sum jv_j=4J-2V. Finally r log2(1/r)<=1/(e ln 2) bounds the remaining
rarity expectation by 2J/(e ln 2). Thus CE<=C_*J. The binary correction
is proved by a complete one-variable derivative sign argument in the
standalone proof. The strict rational constant follows from e>8/3 and
ln 2>2/3, giving 2/(e ln 2)<9/8.

For finite positive output masses, lengths ceil(log2(1/Q_F(S))) obey
Kraft and admit a canonical prefix trie. One TOTAL rounding bit suffices;
I_data=p|A|+q|B|>=1 pays it. Empty-selector atoms, both data labels,
support choices, amplitude rarity, and the global normalizer are all
included. There is no paid-prior choice or hidden surviving leaf label.

Precise failed repair, preserved: passively push forward the old
uniform-mask Kraft law and suppress its leaf record. On the terminal-only
switch its common data atom has reference mass (1-4^-a)/3 but true mass
(1-2^-a)^2. Hence its cross-entropy is at least
(1-2^-a)^2 log2[3/(1-4^-a)] -> log2 3 while J=a2^-a ->0.
Even that output quotient fails selector payment. The repaired generator
must use signed/coherent physical suffix amplitudes, not old uniform
Kraft weights. This does not refute a universal infimum over all codes.

Independent reconstruction of A012's subclaim: for the separate
F_r=1+A_r(y-1), p=2^-r, the control-first tree has D=2(1-p), raw cost 2D,
and actual I=p(r+1). Its raw cost/I is 4(2^r-1)/(r+1) -> infinity.
The Fourier constant is 1-p and all other occupied coefficients have
magnitude p, giving H=2rp(2-p)+L((1-p)^2)<=4rp+2p log2(e). Any exact
witness retaining physical leaf L satisfies
H(W|S)>=H(L|S)>=D-H(F)>=2(1-p)-4rp-2p log2(e) ->2.
This reconstructed objection is accepted at exactly that scope;
REVIEW_DELTA.json records the subclaim, not blanket A012 acceptance.
Our theorem uses two nonempty character labels and is not substituted
for A012's separate constant/Boolean-core activation target.

Arbitrary seed guard: for any Boolean h on disjoint coordinates and
complete fixed Q_h (including empty mass and subprobability cost),
Q_F tensor Q_h is a complete reference for F*h with exact cost
CE_F+CE(P_h,Q_h) and influence I(F)+I(h). For h^t the seed terms are
t CE_h and t I(h). The repaired physical-label cost does not erase that
terminal cost, and no seed FEI bound is assumed.

First unsupported universal line: for a general Boolean selector g,
replace the comb's uniform predecessor law in each max-index group by
a complete conditional reference with a dimension-independent physical
influence payment. Indeed the exact two-character formula is
H(F_g)=L(p^2)+L(q^2)+Var(g)+H_nonempty(g)/2, I_sel(F_g)=I(g)/2.
For balanced g this is 2+H(g)/2. Thus unrestricted two-label selection
retains full-FEI strength. Neither cheap output label names nor physical
disjoint cells pay its missing conditional spectral entropy.

# A011 / B03: prescribed-reference transfer for shared-data phase combs

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Ordinary one-word prefix rounding retains an additive one; biased cores do not automatically pay it. ## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

PHASE_COMB.md is the complete standalone all-dimensional proof. A scalar sign comb with suffix means m_j has r_j=(1-sigma_j m_(j+1))/2. Its max-index-j group has total mass v_j=2^(1-j)r_j^2, uniform predecessor subsets, total V=1-m_1^2, and I=(1/2)sum(j+1)v_j=sum 2^(1-j)r_j. Exact entropy is L(m_1^2)+sum v_j[2(j-1)+2log2(1/r_j)]. L(1-V)<2V for V>0 and r log2(1/r)<=1/(e ln2) give H<=C_*I-2V<=C_*I, C_*=4+2/(e ln2)<41/8. All empty atoms and zero groups are included correctly. This proof does not assume FEI for scalar decision lists.

At every positive data frequency the common-magnitude leaf coefficients are sqrt(P(T)) times Boolean signs. Consequently Fhat(U,T)=sqrt(P(T))*sigma_That(U). Its exact spectrum factors as P(T)K_T(U), with K_T the scalar phase-comb spectrum. Define the full fixed reference Q(U,T)=R(T)K_T(U); at zero true data frequencies choose a normalized empty-selector kernel. Then sum Q=sum R<=1, CE=c+E_P H(K_T), I_sel=E_P I(sigma_T), and I_data=I(g_1). Thus all additional selector cost is influence-paid, independently of core bias and cost; terminal reference deficit is conserved exactly. This is output generation after coherent signed aggregation, not subtraction of physical leaf information from transmitted bits.

For arbitrary leaves, suffix means h_j=(g_j+h_(j+1))/2 give Fhat(U,T)=2^-j(-1)^(|U|-1)(g_j-h_(j+1))hat(T) when max U=j. The data marginal is q=sum w_j P_j. With r=sum w_j R_j, the compatible root reference has exact cost H(U|T)+sum w_j c_j+JS-kappa, where JS=H(q)-sum w_j H(P_j), and kappa is the actual conditional leaf posterior divergence. This conserves reference normalizers and all terminal costs. It is A097's existing transfer written in closed comb coordinates.

The first unsupported universal line is admission/payment when one leaf changes Fourier magnitudes: normalized data coefficient rows are then real rather than Boolean signs. The scalar lemma cannot be applied to them. No one-use magnitude-change reserve, universal selector choice or terminal core influence bound is proved. For arbitrary Boolean label selectors, dictator data leaves recover F=b(x)y, retaining H(b); the universal phase replacement is full-FEI strength. The new conclusion concerns stopped decision-list phases, whereas A019 pays disjoint product and one-shared-bit star phases. No new class/constant priority assertion is made.

# A012 / B03 stage 2: exact posterior-erased activation cost

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

For every balanced Boolean core g on a uniform data cube, every integer r>=1 fresh point controls, and every complete nonempty core spectral subprobability reference R_g of cost c_g, construct a complete control-first posterior-erased output reference for F=1+A_r(g-1), pay all sector/support/normalization costs, and retain the full core cost. Compute the exact posterior of the original true physical-leaf witness for small r and prove the all-r identity. Entropy and cross entropy are in bits; I is ordinary uniform physical total influence.

Resolved at this scope: CE(F)=p*c_g+A_r with p=2^-r and A_r=2rp(2-p)+(1-p)^2log2(1/(1-p)^2). ## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

DERIVATION.md is the complete standalone proof, POSTERIOR_ACTIVATION.tex is the mathematical deliverable, and A013_ACTIVATION_REVIEW.md is the independent review. The critical arguments follow.

The direct output reference gives vacuum mass (1-p)^2, mass p^2 at each nonempty control-only set, and p^2 R_g(T) at each (U,T) with T nonempty. Its total is 1-p+p sum R_g<=1. A sector generator pays the vacuum/control/data probabilities and uniform control support choices explicitly; missing core reference mass is failure. Exact cross entropy is p*c_g+A_r. In fact CE-H(F)=p[c_g-H(g)], so the full reference deficit and terminal spectral cost remain. This is an ideal cross-entropy/reference statement; one-symbol integer prefix rounding is not silently discarded.

The scalar coefficient c_r=A_r/(rp) satisfies c_r<=4+2/(r ln2). From ln2>2/3, every r>=6 has c_r<4.5. Outward rational certificates for r=1,...,5 show c_3>4.5 and c_j<c_3 for j=1,2,4,5. This analytic tail plus finite sign certificates proves the exact all-r maximum at r=3. Exact core references make CE=H(F); Gibbs then proves that no smaller coefficient works in this universal activation transfer inequality. This is not the best full-FEI constant.

For the true witness, at each actual control node sample a fair physical child, its residual spectral set, then membership with theta=(u-v)^2/[2(u^2+v^2)]. Squared output coefficients are exactly (u+v)^2/4 and (u-v)^2/4. The physical leaf distribution has entropy D=2(1-p). At a k-control suffix, all residual nonempty packets have theta=1/2; the residual-empty packet has q_k=[1+(1-2^{-(k-1)})^2]/2 and epsilon_k=2^{-2(k-1)}/[2(1+(1-2^{-(k-1)})^2)]. The local membership entropy is B_k=q_k h2(epsilon_k)+(1-m_k^2)/2. Chain rule yields the exact formula for H(W|S), with every core entropy term cancelling only between H(W) and H(S), not from the paid reference cost. W is exactly (physical leaf J, output S), so D-A_r<=H(W|S)<=D and the limit is two. Raw control masses exceed p^2, proving the original h2(v)+2rv+p*c_g bound and coefficient 6+2/ln2. No mathematical correction to A013's original activation proof is needed. Our sharp direct-sector reference is a separate refinement, not a falsely asserted improvement of the old reference's own cost.
